//! Role authority is compared by effective gates, never by names like 'admin'.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#123-actionable-authority-advice — advisory grant-edge analysis, not a security proof or implicit exemption.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn probe_pairs(bound:&Bound,table:&str,index:&str,key:&[RuleTerm],equalities:&[(String,RuleTerm)])->Vec<(String,RuleTerm)>{
    let mut pairs=equalities.to_vec();
    if let Some(idx)=bound.schema.table(table).and_then(|t|t.index(index)){for (component,term) in idx.components.iter().zip(key){if let Some(column)=component.column(){pairs.push((column.into(),term.clone()));}}}
    pairs
}
pub(super) fn global_principal(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->Option<String>{
    let t=bound.schema.table(table)?;
    t.indexes.values().find_map(|index|{
        if !index.unique||index.components.len()!=1{return None;}
        let column=index.components[0].column()?;
        matches!(t.columns.get(column),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)).then(||column.to_string())
    })
}
pub(super) fn membership_probe(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str)->bool{
    let RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..}=rule else{return false;};
    if table!=roster{return false;}
    let Some(principal)=global_principal(bound,table)else{return false;};
    let pairs=probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
    pairs.len()==1&&pairs[0].0==principal&&pairs[0].1==RuleTerm::Viewer
}
// @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — ownership is required for every supplied owned field;
// only an exclusively owned patch bypasses an unnamed row update gate.
fn write_admission(table:&snapback4_core::schema::Table,op:ast::WriteOp,fields:&[ast::ObjectField])->RuleExpr{
    let touched=fields.iter().filter(|f|!f.spread&&f.name!="id").map(|f|f.name.as_str()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let owned_only=!touched.is_empty()&&!fields.iter().any(|f|f.spread)&&touched.iter().all(|f|table.written_by.contains_key(*f));
    let gate=match op{
        ast::WriteOp::Insert=>table.rules.insert.clone(),
        ast::WriteOp::Update if owned_only&&!table.update_transition=>RuleExpr::Allow,
        ast::WriteOp::Update=>table.update_rule_judged().into_owned(),
        _=>RuleExpr::Deny,
    };
    let mut owners=table.written_by.iter().filter(|(field,authority)|touched.contains(field.as_str())||op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&!authority.audience&&table.rules.update.needs_field_value(field,&table.columns[*field])).map(|(_,authority)|RuleExpr::Eq(if op==ast::WriteOp::Insert{RuleTerm::Field(authority.owner.clone())}else{RuleTerm::OldField(authority.owner.clone())},RuleTerm::Viewer)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    owners.push(RuleExpr::Or(gate.disjuncts().iter().filter(|r|r.service_grant().is_none()).map(|r|(*r).clone()).collect()));
    RuleExpr::And(owners)
}
pub(super) fn membership_grant_risk(bound:&Bound,roster:&str,operation:&str,requirements:&[RuleExpr],op:ast::WriteOp,fields:&[ast::ObjectField])->bool{
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert{return membership_grant_risk(bound,roster,operation,requirements,ast::WriteOp::Insert,fields)||membership_grant_risk(bound,roster,operation,requirements,ast::WriteOp::Update,fields);}
    fn honors(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str)->bool{match rule{
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|r|honors(bound,r,roster)),
        r=>membership_probe(bound,r,roster),
    }}
    if global_principal(bound,roster).is_none()||!bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|honors(bound,r,roster))){return false;}
    let table=&bound.schema.tables[roster];
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(std::iter::once(write_admission(table,op,fields)).chain(requirements.iter().cloned()).collect());
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&global_principal(bound,roster).is_some_and(|column|kernel::implies(&admission,&RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::OldField(column.clone()),RuleTerm::Field(column)))==Ok(true)){return false;}
    let ordinary=substitute(bound,&admission,"$no-roster","$membership",None,None);
    bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==roster&&i.column.is_none()&&(i.operation.as_deref()==Some(operation)||i.operation.is_none()&&(i.target=="write"||i.target==if op==ast::WriteOp::Insert{"insert"}else{"update"}))).filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).any(|p|super::operation::safe(p)&&kernel::implies(&ordinary,p)==Ok(false))
}
fn substitute(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str,column:&str,role:Option<&str>,target:Option<&str>)->RuleExpr{
    let known=|t:&RuleTerm|match t{RuleTerm::Field(c)|RuleTerm::NextField(c) if c==column=>target.map(|s|snapback4_core::Value::String(s.into())),RuleTerm::Literal(v)=>Some(v.clone()),_=>None};
    match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,index,equalities,..} if table==roster&&key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{
            let pairs=probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
            let requested=pairs.iter().find(|(c,_)|c==column).and_then(|(_,t)|if let RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(s))=t{Some(s.as_str())}else{None});
            if requested.is_none(){rule.clone()}else if requested==role{
                if pairs.iter().all(|(c,t)|c==column||matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer)){RuleExpr::Allow}else{rule.clone()}
            }else{RuleExpr::Deny}
        },
        RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Id(seat)))|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Id(seat)),RuleTerm::Viewer) if seat.starts_with("service:")=>RuleExpr::Deny,
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(known(a),known(b)){if (a==b)==matches!(rule,RuleExpr::Eq(..)){RuleExpr::Allow}else{RuleExpr::Deny}}else{rule.clone()},
        RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
        RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
        RuleExpr::Not(r)=>RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target))),
        _=>rule.clone(),
    }
}
pub(super) fn risk(bound:&Bound,roster:&str,column:&str,operation:&str,targets:&[String],op:ast::WriteOp,requirements:&[RuleExpr],dynamic:bool,fields:&[ast::ObjectField])->Option<String>{
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert{return risk(bound,roster,column,operation,targets,ast::WriteOp::Insert,requirements,dynamic,fields).or_else(||risk(bound,roster,column,operation,targets,ast::WriteOp::Update,requirements,dynamic,fields));}
    let table=bound.schema.table(roster)?;
    if requirements.iter().any(|r|!r.disjuncts().is_empty()&&r.disjuncts().iter().all(|b|b.service_grant().is_some())){return None;}
    let roles=match table.columns.get(column)?.required(){snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Enum{variants}=>variants.clone(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Text{..}=>{
        let mut roles=BTreeSet::new();
        fn collect(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str,column:&str,roles:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
            RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if table==roster=>for (c,t) in &probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities){if c==column&&let RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(s))=t{roles.insert(s.clone());}},
            RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{collect(bound,r,roster,column,roles)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>collect(bound,r,roster,column,roles),_=>{}
        }}
        for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{collect(bound,r,roster,column,&mut roles);}}roles.into_iter().collect()
    },_=>return None};
    let intents=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==roster&&i.column.as_deref().is_none_or(|c|c==column)&&(i.operation.as_deref()==Some(operation)||i.operation.is_none()&&(i.target=="write"||i.target==if op==ast::WriteOp::Insert{"insert"}else{"update"}))).filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    fn role_guards(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,out:&mut Vec<RuleExpr>){match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))&&probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,v)|role_columns(bound,table).contains(c)&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_))))=>out.push(rule.clone()),
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{role_guards(bound,r,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(std::iter::once(write_admission(table,op,fields)).chain(requirements.iter().cloned()).collect());
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&kernel::implies(&admission,&RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::OldField(column.into()),RuleTerm::Field(column.into())))==Ok(true){return None;}
    let mut guards=Vec::new();role_guards(bound,&admission,&mut guards);
    let independent=kernel::implies(&substitute(bound,&admission,"$no-roster",column,None,None),&RuleExpr::Or(guards))==Ok(true);
    for target in roles.iter().filter(|r|targets.is_empty()||targets.contains(r)){
        for caller in std::iter::once(None).chain(roles.iter().map(|s|Some(s.as_str()))){
            if caller==Some(target.as_str()){continue;}
            let admitted=substitute(bound,&admission,roster,column,caller,Some(target));
            let admits=kernel::implies(&admitted,&RuleExpr::Deny)!=Ok(true);
            if !admits{continue;}
            let violates_intent=intents.iter().any(|p|kernel::implies(&admitted,&substitute(bound,p,roster,column,caller,Some(target)))==Ok(false));
            // Existing independently gated appointers may delegate different
            // roles. Independent intent can restrict a particular target role.
            if independent&&!violates_intent{continue;}
            if violates_intent{return Some(format!("{roster}.{column} assignment to '{target}' is not established by the independent delegation intent for {}",caller.unwrap_or("a caller without a role")));}
            for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){for (effect,rule) in [("read",&t.rules.read),("insert",&t.rules.insert),("update",&t.rules.update),("delete",&t.rules.delete)]{
                let granted=substitute(bound,rule,roster,column,Some(target),None);let previous=substitute(bound,rule,roster,column,caller,None);
                if kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,&granted)!=Ok(true){continue;}
                if !dynamic&&!(matches!(effect,"read"|"delete")||!role_columns(bound,name).is_empty()){continue;}
                if kernel::implies(&granted,&previous)==Ok(false){return Some(format!("{} can confer '{target}', which adds {name}.{effect} authority not held by {}",roster,caller.unwrap_or("a caller without a role")));}
            }}
        }
    }None
}

pub(super) fn role_columns(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut columns=BTreeSet::new();
    if let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table){for c in ["role","kind"]{if t.columns.contains_key(c){columns.insert(c.into());}}}
    fn collect(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,table:&str,columns:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table:t,index,key,equalities,..} if t==table&&key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>for (c,v) in &probe_pairs(bound,t,index,key,equalities){if matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_))){columns.insert(c.clone());}},
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{collect(bound,r,table,columns)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>collect(bound,r,table,columns),_=>{}
    }}
    let named=columns.clone();let mut observed=BTreeSet::new();
    for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{collect(bound,r,table,&mut observed);}}
    let global=bound.schema.table(table).is_some_and(|t|t.indexes.values().any(|index|{
        if !index.unique{return false;}
        let Some(parts)=index.components.iter().map(|p|p.column()).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()else{return false;};
        let principals=parts.iter().filter(|c|matches!(t.columns.get(**c).map(|k|k.required()),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal))).count();
        if principals!=1{return false;}
        parts.iter().all(|c|matches!(t.columns.get(*c).map(|k|k.required()),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal))||named.contains(*c))
            ||named.is_empty()&&observed.len()==1&&parts.len()==2&&parts.iter().all(|c|observed.contains(*c)||matches!(t.columns.get(*c).map(|k|k.required()),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)))
    }));
    if global{for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{collect(bound,r,table,&mut columns);}}}else{columns.clear();}
    columns
}
//! Expand advisory write sites with lexical arguments and dominating guards.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#128-runtime-intent-and-independent-worlds — helper placement cannot hide a grant writer.
use super::*;
use ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramDecl,FunctionBody};
type Env=BTreeMap<String,Expr>;
fn resolve(expr:&Expr,env:&Env)->Expr{
    let mut expr=expr.clone();
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|{
        if let Expr::Object{fields,..}|Expr::Write{fields,..}=e{for f in fields{if f.value.is_none(){f.value=Some(env.get(&f.name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Expr::Name{name:f.name.clone(),site:f.site.clone()}));}}}
        if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e&&let Some(value)=env.get(name){*e=value.clone();}
    });expr
}
fn expression(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,guards:&[Stmt],depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    let mut expr=resolve(expr,env);
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|match e{
        Expr::Write{..}=>{let mut context=p.clone();context.prelude=Vec::new();context.body=guards.to_vec();out.push((e.clone(),context));},
        Expr::Call{name,args,..} if depth<32=>if let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(name){
            let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(arg,value)|(arg.name.clone(),value.clone())).collect();
            let mut inherited=guards.to_vec();
            match &f.body{
                FunctionBody::Expression(value)=>expression(catalog,p,value,&locals,&inherited,depth+1,out),
                FunctionBody::Block(body)=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut locals,&mut inherited,depth+1,out),
            }
        },_=>{}
    });
}
// Only an unconditional direct call exports a helper's guaranteed prefix.
// Conditional/loop-local requirements never become caller assumptions.
fn helper_guards(catalog:&Catalog,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,depth:usize)->Vec<Stmt>{
    if depth>=32{return Vec::new();}
    let Expr::Call{name,args,..}=resolve(expr,env)else{return Vec::new();};
    let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(&name)else{return Vec::new();};
    let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(a,v)|(a.name.clone(),v)).collect::<Env>();
    let FunctionBody::Block(body)=&f.body else{return if let FunctionBody::Expression(value)=&f.body{helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)}else{Vec::new()};};
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for s in body{match s{
        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>out.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,&locals),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()}),
        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));if names.len()==1{locals.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,&locals));}},
        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)),
        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));break;},_=>break,
    }}out
}
fn statements(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,body:&[Stmt],env:&mut Env,guards:&mut Vec<Stmt>,depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    for s in body{match s{
        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));if names.len()==1{env.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,env));}},
        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>{
            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,depth,out);
            guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,env),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()});
        },
        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);break;},
        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));},
        Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,..}=>{
            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,depth,out);
            statements(catalog,p,then_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);
            statements(catalog,p,else_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);
        },
        Stmt::For{domain,name,body,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,domain,env,guards,depth,out);let mut local=env.clone();local.remove(name);statements(catalog,p,body,&mut local,&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);},
        Stmt::As{body,..}=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out),_=>{}
    }}
}
pub(super) fn writes(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut env=Env::new();let mut guards=Vec::new();
    statements(catalog,p,&p.prelude,&mut env,&mut guards,0,&mut out);
    statements(catalog,p,&p.body,&mut env,&mut guards,0,&mut out);out
}
//! Advice identifies a risk shape; it never supplies a proof assumption.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#123-actionable-authority-advice — current roles, grant writers, required evidence and refusal oracles.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramKind};
const NOW_FOR_DEFAULTS:i64=1_800_000_000_000;
fn rule_parts(rule:&RuleExpr)->Vec<&RuleExpr>{match rule{RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().flat_map(rule_parts).collect(),RuleExpr::Not(x)=>rule_parts(x),r=>vec![r]}}
fn role_test(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {rule_parts(rule).iter().any(|r|match r{
    RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{
        if super::privilege::membership_probe(bound,r,table){return true;}
        let columns=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
        super::privilege::probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,t)|columns.contains(c)&&matches!(t,RuleTerm::Literal(_)))
    },_=>false,
})}
fn reader_columns(rule:&RuleExpr)->BTreeSet<String>{rule_parts(rule).into_iter().filter_map(|r|match r{RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(f))=>Some(f.clone()),_=>None}).collect()}
fn note(notes:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>,site:&Site,code:&str,message:String,rewrite:&str){notes.push(Diagnostic::new(site,code,message,rewrite).guide("security"));}
fn mutable_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str)->bool {
    let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(name)else{return false;};
    let mut roles=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name);
    if roles.is_empty()&&let Some(principal)=super::privilege::global_principal(bound,name){roles.insert(principal);}
    if roles.is_empty(){return false;}
    let mutable=roles.iter().any(|c|!t.constraints.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(c))));
    fn supplied(value:&serde_json::Value,roles:&BTreeSet<String>)->bool{
        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Object").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Literal").and_then(|v|v.get("Map").or_else(||v.get("Row"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
        true // Dynamic patches cannot establish that role fields are untouched.
    }
    fn writes(value:&serde_json::Value,name:&str,roles:&BTreeSet<String>,update:bool,delete:bool)->bool{
        match value{
            serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>{
                for tag in ["Update","Upsert","WriteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
                    let target=if tag=="WriteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
                    if update&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name)&&body.get(if tag=="Upsert"{"row"}else{"patch"}).is_some_and(|v|supplied(v,roles)){return true;}
                }}
                for tag in ["Delete","DeleteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
                    let target=if tag=="DeleteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
                    if delete&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name){return true;}
                }}
                xs.values().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete))
            },serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete)),_=>false,
        }
    }
    bound.programs.iter().any(|p|{
        let system=p.program.kind==snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job;
        let update=mutable&&(system||!matches!(t.rules.update,RuleExpr::Deny)||roles.iter().any(|c|t.written_by.contains_key(c)));
        let delete=system||!matches!(t.rules.delete,RuleExpr::Deny);
        writes(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),name,&roles,update,delete)
    })
}
fn recipient_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str,branch:&RuleExpr,grant:Option<&str>,roster:&str,effect:&str)->bool{
    if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==*name&&(i.target==effect||i.target=="write"&&matches!(effect,"insert"|"update"))&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::dependencies(p).contains(roster)&&role_test(bound,p)&&kernel::implies(branch,p)!=Ok(true))){return true;}
    let _=(branch,grant,roster);
    // Field names cannot establish a current-role contract. Without independent
    // intent, retained access may be deliberate, so lifetime advice stays silent.
    false
}
pub(super) fn notes(bound:&Bound,exposures:&[Exposure])->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let mut notes=Vec::new();
    for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
        if t.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable(){note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_BROAD_READ",format!("{name} is readable by every signed-in user: {}",t.rules.read.quarry()),"confirm the spec permits these columns and declare read intent; snapback4 audit lists inherited grants and their writers");}
        if let RuleExpr::Exists{table:roster,key,equalities,..}=&t.rules.read
            && equalities.is_empty()&&key.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer)) {
            note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_BROAD_READ",format!("{name} is readable by any signed-in user with a matching {roster} row; no particular role or row ownership is required"),"confirm that every roster member should see all these columns; declare intent from the privacy spec and inspect audit");
        }
        for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
            use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType as C;
            if kind.contains_asset() && (kind.is_optional() || matches!(kind.required(),C::List{..}|C::ListRange{min:0,..})) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE",format!("{name}.{column} permits omitted or empty media evidence"),"if the spec requires evidence, declare a required image/file or [image] 1..N; otherwise retain optionality deliberately (guide security)");
            }
        }
        for leaf in rule_parts(&t.rules.insert) {
            if let RuleExpr::Created {table:parent,..}=leaf
                && bound.schema.table(parent).is_some_and(|p| p.rules.insert.disjuncts().iter().any(|r| matches!(r,RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(_),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(_)))))
                && bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other| [&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete].iter().any(|r| rule_parts(r).iter().any(|r|matches!(r,RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,equalities,..} if table==name&&matches!(key.as_slice(),[RuleTerm::Viewer])&&equalities.is_empty())))) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_VACUOUS_RULE",format!("{name}: `created {parent}` can bootstrap authority trusted globally by a viewer-only probe; a caller may create their own global administrator grant"),"provision global authority only through an operator/service seat or existing authorized admin; group creation must scope every later grant to that group (guide deploy, first-admin setup)");
            }
        }
        for (effect,rule) in [("read",&t.rules.read),("insert",&t.rules.insert),("update",&t.rules.update),("delete",&t.rules.delete)] {
          for branch in rule.disjuncts(){
            for dep in kernel::dependencies(branch){if let Some(g)=bound.schema.table(&dep) {
                let rosters=[&g.rules.insert,&g.rules.update].into_iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).filter(|r|r!=&dep&&mutable_role(bound,r)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
                if rosters.iter().any(|r|recipient_role(bound,name,branch,Some(&dep),r,effect))&&(role_test(bound,&g.rules.insert)||role_test(bound,&g.rules.update)){
                    note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE",format!("{name}.{effect}: `{}` trusts retained {dep} grants while current role in {} can change",branch.quarry(),rosters.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),"recheck the current role in this branch or revoke every derived grant transactionally on role changes; guide security");
                }
            }}
            let declared=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==*name&&(i.target==effect||i.target=="write"&&matches!(effect,"insert"|"update"))).filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).flat_map(kernel::dependencies).filter(|r|mutable_role(bound,r)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
            if !reader_columns(branch).is_empty()&&declared.iter().any(|r|recipient_role(bound,name,branch,None,r,effect)) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE",format!("{name}.{effect}: principal branch `{}` does not recheck the declared mutable current role",branch.quarry()),"recheck the current role as well as principal attribution; guide security");
            }
          }
        }
    }
    // Inspect original syntax for argument flow and distinct authored codes.
    // Keeping lints outside Bound.notes preserves the compiler's existing
    // diagnostic contract; check/audit expose this security advice separately.
    let _=exposures;
    let mut retained=BTreeSet::new();
    notes.retain(|n|n.code!="N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE"||retained.insert(n.message.split(':').next().unwrap_or("").to_string()));
    notes
}
pub(super) fn source_notes(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    let documents=sources.iter().map(|(file,source)|crate::parse_document(file,source).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let catalog=super::super::catalog::Catalog::build(documents.clone(),&mut Vec::new());
    for doc in documents{
        for p in &doc.programs {
            let mut body=p.prelude.clone();body.extend(p.body.clone());
            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
            let mut guarded=BTreeSet::new();
            for statement in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body) {
                if let Stmt::Require{condition,..}=statement {
                    let mut condition=condition.clone();
                    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut condition,&mut |e|if let Expr::Exists{read,..}=e {
                        for key in &read.keys {if let Expr::Name{name,..}=&key.value {guarded.insert(name.clone());}}
                    });
                }
            }
            for (write,context) in super::authority_walk::writes(&catalog,p){
                let Expr::Write{op,table,fields,site,..}=&write else{continue;};
                let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table)else{continue;};
                let role_columns=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
                let principal=super::privilege::global_principal(bound,table);
                let reassigns=principal.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==c)&&!t.constraints.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(c))));
                if role_columns.is_empty()&&p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(matches!(op,ast::WriteOp::Insert|ast::WriteOp::Upsert)||*op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&reassigns){
                    let operation=super::super::operation_name(std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy().as_ref(),&p.name);
                    let requirements=catalog.tables.get(table).map(|info|super::operation::requirements(&catalog,info,&context)).unwrap_or_default();
                    if super::privilege::membership_grant_risk(bound,table,&operation,&requirements,if *op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert&&!reassigns{ast::WriteOp::Insert}else{*op},fields)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),"$membership".into())){
                        note(&mut out,site,"N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION",format!("{}: {table} membership grants authority, but assignment does not establish the independent provisioning intent",p.name),"restrict membership grants to the declared provisioner; use guide staging's first-admin recipe");
                    }
                }
                for column in &role_columns {if p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==column)||matches!(op,ast::WriteOp::Insert|ast::WriteOp::Upsert)&&t.defaults.contains_key(column))&&bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other|[&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(table))) {
                    let mut targets=fields.iter().filter(|f|&f.name==column).filter_map(|f|match &f.value{Some(Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(s),..})=>Some(s.clone()),_=>None}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                    if !fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==column)&&let Some(snapback4_core::Value::String(role))=t.defaults.get(column).map(|d|d.value(NOW_FOR_DEFAULTS,"$advisory")){targets.push(role);}
                    let chosen=fields.iter().find(|f|&f.name==column);
                    let dynamic=chosen.map_or(targets.is_empty(),|f|!matches!(f.value,Some(Expr::Literal{..})));
                    if let Some(field)=chosen {
                        if let Some(Expr::Name{name:argument,..})=&field.value&&let Some(arg)=p.args.iter().find(|a|a.name==*argument)&&let ast::TypeKind::Enum{variants}= &arg.type_.kind {targets=variants.clone();}
                    }
                    let requirements=catalog.tables.get(table).map(|info|super::operation::requirements(&catalog,info,&context)).unwrap_or_default();
                    let effective_op=if chosen.is_none()&&*op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert{ast::WriteOp::Insert}else{*op};
                    if let Some(risk)=super::privilege::risk(bound,table,column,&super::super::operation_name(std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy().as_ref(),&p.name),&targets,effective_op,&requirements,dynamic,fields)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),format!("$privilege:{column}"))) {
                        note(&mut out,site,"N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION",format!("{}: {risk}; review the grant's delegation policy",p.name),"restrict role assignment to authorized delegation or an operator seat; use guide staging's first-admin recipe (intentional open enrollment may permit this)");
                    }
                }}
            }
            super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body,&mut |e|{
                let Expr::Write{table,fields,site,..}=e else{return};
                let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table)else{return};
                let readers=reader_columns(&t.rules.read);
                for f in fields.iter(){
                    let arg=f.value.as_ref().and_then(|e|if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e{Some(name.as_str())}else{None}).unwrap_or(f.name.as_str());
                    if !guarded.contains(arg)&&readers.contains(&f.name)&&p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==arg)&&seen.insert((table.clone(),f.name.clone())){
                        note(&mut out,site,"N_ARGUMENT_GRANT",format!("{} writes argument {arg} straight into reader-grant column {table}.{}",p.name,f.name),"require the chosen principal's current role and relationship before granting access; caller authority alone does not validate the recipient");
                    }

                }
            });
            // Distinct absent and denial codes disclose logical existence to
            // callers admitted by a broader table rule, even without payload.
            fn refusals(body:&[Stmt],bound:&Bound,absent:&mut Option<String>,codes:&mut Vec<(Site,String)>){for s in body{match s{
                Stmt::Bind{value:Expr::Assert{value,code,..},..}=>{
                    *absent=None;
                    if let Expr::Read(read)=value.as_ref()&&let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(&read.table){
                        let own=read.keys.iter().any(|k|matches!(&k.value,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"));
                        if !t.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable()&&!own {
                            // A table read gate already conflates hidden and absent.
                            // Only broader program-level privacy intent can make
                            // its later refusal distinguish private existence.
                            if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==read.table&&i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|policy|kernel::implies(&t.rules.read,policy)==Ok(false))){*absent=Some(code.clone());}
                        }
                    }
                },
                Stmt::Require{site,code,..}=>if let Some(a)=absent.as_ref()&&a!=code{codes.push((site.clone(),format!("lookup absence {a}, later denial {code}")));},
                Stmt::If{then_body,else_body,..}=>{refusals(then_body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes);refusals(else_body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes);},
                Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>refusals(body,bound,&mut absent.clone(),codes),_=>{}}}}
            let mut codes=Vec::new();refusals(&p.body,bound,&mut None,&mut codes);
            for (site,why) in codes.into_iter().take(1){note(&mut out,&site,"N_EXISTENCE_ORACLE",format!("{}: {why}; row readership is broader than declared private-existence intent",p.name),"use the same absence/denial code and message for private existence, or tighten the row audience");}
            if p.kind==ProgramKind::Job {
                fn guards(body:&[Stmt],out:&mut Vec<Expr>){for s in body {match s{
                    Stmt::Require{condition,..}=>out.push(condition.clone()),
                    Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,..}=>{out.push(condition.clone());guards(then_body,out);guards(else_body,out);},
                    Stmt::For{body,..}|Stmt::As{body,..}=>guards(body,out),_=>{}
                }}}
                let mut job_guards=Vec::new();guards(&p.body,&mut job_guards);
                let clock=job_guards.iter().any(|g|{let mut g=g.clone();let mut found=false;super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut g,&mut |e|if matches!(e,Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now"){found=true;});found});
                if !clock{continue;}
                let mut writes=BTreeSet::new();let mut b=p.body.clone();
                super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut b,&mut |e|if let Expr::Write{table,fields,..}=e&&fields.iter().any(|f|f.name=="status"){writes.insert(table.clone());});
                for table in writes {for m in doc.programs.iter().filter(|m|m.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation){
                    let mut aliases=BTreeSet::new();
                    for s in &m.body{if let Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=s{
                        let v=if let Expr::Assert{value,..}=value{value.as_ref()}else{value};
                        if matches!(v,Expr::Read(r) if r.table==table){aliases.extend(names.clone());}
                    }}
                    let mut checks=Vec::new();guards(&m.body,&mut checks);let(mut status,mut now)=(false,false);
                    for mut c in checks{super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut c,&mut |e|match e{
                        Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now"=>now=true,
                        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if name=="status"&&matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Name{name,..} if aliases.contains(name))=>status=true,_=>{}
                    });}
                    let module = std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().and_then(|s| s.to_str()).unwrap_or("schema");
                    let job_name = if module == "schema" { p.name.clone() } else { format!("{module}.{}", p.name) };
                    let already_named = bound.notes.iter().any(|n| n.code == "N_SWEEP_DEADLINE" && n.site == m.site
                        && n.message.contains(&format!("column {table}.status)"))
                        && n.message.contains(&format!("scheduled job {job_name} ")));
                    if status&&!now&&!already_named{note(&mut out,&m.site,"N_SWEEP_ONLY_DEADLINE",format!("{} gates on {table}.status while clock job {} changes that status; writes can run between the deadline and sweep",m.name,p.name),"enforce the deadline against server now in the mutation too; a sweep cleans up state but does not enforce an exact deadline");}
                }}
            }
        }
    }
    out
}

//! Narrow stable leading-require reduction for operation-specific authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#81-exact-conservative-boundary-review-clarification — unknown write sets never establish stability.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Stmt,ProgramDecl,ProgramKind,InvariantDecl,RuleDecl};
use super::super::catalog::TableInfo;
pub(super) fn resolve<'a>(catalog:&'a Catalog,decl:&InvariantDecl)->Option<(String,&'a ProgramDecl)>{
    if matches!(decl.target.as_str(),"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"always"|"write"){return None;}
    let matches=catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|{let module=std::path::Path::new(&d.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy();d.programs.iter().map(move|p|(super::super::operation_name(&module,&p.name),p))})
        .filter(|(name,p)|p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(name==&decl.target||p.name==decl.target)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if matches.len()==1 {matches.into_iter().next()}else{None}
}
pub(super) fn safe(rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {match rule{
    RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..}=>key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().all(safe),
    RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>[a,b].iter().all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Deny|RuleExpr::Public{..}=>true,_=>false,
}}
pub(super) fn requirements(catalog:&Catalog,info:&TableInfo,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<RuleExpr>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for s in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body){let Stmt::Require{condition,site,..}=s else{break};
        let decl=RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),value:condition.clone(),site:site.clone()};
        if let Ok(rule)=super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&decl)&&safe(&rule){out.push(rule);}
    }out
}
pub(super) fn prove(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<bool,String>{
    let p=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|Some(&p.program.name)==inv.operation.as_ref()).ok_or("operation did not bind")?;
    if !safe(inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("unsupported policy")?){return Err("operation policy needs unsupported target-image, negative-probe or live-reference bindings".into());}
    let writes=snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program);
    if !writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table){return Err("operation has no direct write to the named table; implicit effects require a reduction".into());}
    let dependencies=inv.requirements.iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|dependencies.contains(t)) {return Err("require dependency can be changed by this operation, including its target write".into());}
    if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.maintained.is_empty()||!t.sequences.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())) || !bound.schema.maintains.is_empty(){return Err("implicit maintain/sequence/referential writes require dependency stability reduction".into());}
    // Source helpers/control flow may hide write sets or viewer changes.
    let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();
    fn opaque(v:&serde_json::Value)->bool{match v{serde_json::Value::Object(m)=>m.keys().any(|k|matches!(k.as_str(),"For"|"If"|"JobAs"|"JobQuery"|"Emit"))||m.values().any(opaque),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(opaque),_=>false}}
    if opaque(&json){return Err("operation control flow or opaque effects are outside stable leading-require reduction".into());}
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(inv.requirements.clone());
    // Table-rule gates can only further restrict a dominating stable require;
    // using the require alone overapproximates effective owner-field gates.
    kernel::implies(&admission,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())
}
//! Finite three-valued implication, deliberately independent of database facts.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#3-sound-proof-boundary — impossible valuations overapproximate all snapshots.
use snapback4_core::schema::{RuleExpr as R, RuleTerm as T};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

pub const MAX_ATOMS: usize = 10;
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Truth { Yes, No, Unknown }
impl Truth {
    fn not(self) -> Self { match self { Self::Yes => Self::No, Self::No => Self::Yes, Self::Unknown => Self::Unknown } }
    fn combine(self, other: Self, and: bool) -> Self {
        let decisive = if and { Self::No } else { Self::Yes };
        if self == decisive || other == decisive { decisive }
        else if self == Self::Unknown || other == Self::Unknown { Self::Unknown }
        else { decisive.not() }
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
enum Formula { Constant(Truth), Atom(usize), Not(Box<Self>), And(Vec<Self>), Or(Vec<Self>) }
impl Formula {
    fn eval(&self, values: &[Truth]) -> Truth {
        match self {
            Self::Constant(v) => *v, Self::Atom(n) => values[*n], Self::Not(x) => x.eval(values).not(),
            Self::And(xs) => xs.iter().fold(Truth::Yes, |v, x| v.combine(x.eval(values), true)),
            Self::Or(xs) => xs.iter().fold(Truth::No, |v, x| v.combine(x.eval(values), false)),
        }
    }
}
fn term(t: &T) -> T { match t { T::NextField(f) => T::Field(f.clone()), other => other.clone() } }
fn atom(rule: &R) -> String {
    let mut r = rule.clone();
    match &mut r {
        R::Eq(a,b) | R::Compare(_,a,b) => { *a=term(a); *b=term(b); }
        R::Exists { key, equalities, .. } | R::Created { key, equalities, .. } => {
            for t in key { *t=term(t); }
            for (_,t) in equalities.iter_mut() { *t=term(t); }
            equalities.sort_by(|a,b| a.0.cmp(&b.0));
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    if let R::Eq(a,b)=&mut r && serde_json::to_string(a).unwrap()>serde_json::to_string(b).unwrap() { std::mem::swap(a,b); }
    let mut json=serde_json::to_value(&r).unwrap();
    // Provenance controls planning, not rule evaluation. Remove only this
    // leaf's metadata, never a similarly named literal-object field.
    for name in ["Exists","Created"] { if let Some(body)=json.get_mut(name).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object_mut) { body.remove("provenance"); } }
    json.to_string()
}
fn formula(rule: &R, atoms: &mut Vec<String>, nodes: &mut usize) -> Result<Formula, String> {
    *nodes+=1; if *nodes>1024 { return Err("formula exceeds 1024 nodes".into()); }
    Ok(match rule {
        R::Allow | R::Public {..} => Formula::Constant(Truth::Yes), R::Deny => Formula::Constant(Truth::No),
        R::And(xs) => Formula::And(xs.iter().map(|r| formula(r,atoms,nodes)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
        R::Or(xs) => Formula::Or(xs.iter().map(|r| formula(r,atoms,nodes)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
        R::Not(x) => Formula::Not(Box::new(formula(x,atoms,nodes)?)),
        R::Ne(a,b) => Formula::Not(Box::new(formula(&R::Eq(a.clone(),b.clone()),atoms,nodes)?)),
        R::Eq(T::Literal(a),T::Literal(b)) => Formula::Constant(if a==b {Truth::Yes}else{Truth::No}),
        R::Eq(T::Viewer,T::Viewer) => Formula::Constant(Truth::Yes),
        other => { let key=atom(other); let n=atoms.iter().position(|a| a==&key).unwrap_or_else(|| {atoms.push(key);atoms.len()-1});
            if atoms.len()>MAX_ATOMS {return Err(format!("more than {MAX_ATOMS} independent predicates"));} Formula::Atom(n) }
    })
}
/// `Ok(true)` is a proof; `Ok(false)` is only an abstract countermodel.
/// Neither limit exhaustion nor a countermodel is a concrete refutation.
pub fn implies(admission: &R, policy: &R) -> Result<bool,String> {
    let (mut atoms,mut nodes)=(Vec::new(),0);
    let a=formula(admission,&mut atoms,&mut nodes)?;
    let p=formula(policy,&mut atoms,&mut nodes)?;
    let mut values=vec![Truth::Unknown;atoms.len()];
    for mut n in 0..3usize.pow(atoms.len() as u32) {
        for v in &mut values { *v=match n%3 {0=>Truth::Yes,1=>Truth::No,_=>Truth::Unknown}; n/=3; }
        if a.eval(&values)==Truth::Yes && p.eval(&values)!=Truth::Yes { return Ok(false); }
    }
    Ok(true)
}
pub fn dependencies(rule: &R) -> BTreeSet<String> {
    let mut out=BTreeSet::new();
    match rule {
        R::Exists{table,key,equalities,..}|R::Created{table,key,equalities,..} => {out.insert(table.clone());for term in key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)){for (table,_) in term.references(){out.insert(table.into());}}}
        R::And(xs)|R::Or(xs) => for x in xs {out.extend(dependencies(x));}, R::Not(x) => out.extend(dependencies(x)),
        R::Eq(a,b)|R::Ne(a,b)|R::Compare(_,a,b) => for t in [a,b] { for (table,_) in t.references() {out.insert(table.into());} },
        _=>{}
    }
    out
}
#[cfg(test)] mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn a() -> R { R::Eq(T::Field("owner".into()),T::Viewer) }
    #[test] fn kleene_unknown_is_neither_true_nor_false() {
        assert_eq!(Truth::Unknown.not(),Truth::Unknown);
        assert_eq!(Truth::No.combine(Truth::Unknown,true),Truth::No);
        assert_eq!(Truth::Yes.combine(Truth::Unknown,false),Truth::Yes);
        assert!(!implies(&R::Allow,&R::Or(vec![a(),R::Not(Box::new(a()))])).unwrap());
    }
    #[test] fn no_boolean_assignment_is_a_concrete_refutation() {
        assert!(implies(&R::And(vec![a(),R::Deny]),&R::Deny).unwrap());
        assert!(!implies(&R::Allow,&a()).unwrap());
        assert!(implies(&a(),&R::Eq(T::Viewer,T::Field("owner".into()))).unwrap());
        let xs=(0..11).map(|n|R::Eq(T::Field(format!("f{n}")),T::Viewer)).collect();
        assert!(implies(&R::And(xs),&a()).is_err());
    }
}
//! Partial typed row-policy interpreter, never used to establish a proof.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#81-exact-conservative-boundary-review-clarification — unknown/unsupported is not false.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Expr,Literal};
use snapback4_core::Value;
type Rows=BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>;
fn boolean(v:Option<Value>)->Option<bool>{match v{Some(Value::Bool(v))=>Some(v),_=>None}}
pub(super) fn evaluate(expr:&Expr,row:&Value,rows:&Rows,schema:&Schema,viewer:&str)->Option<bool>{boolean(value(expr,row,rows,schema,viewer))}
fn value(e:&Expr,row:&Value,rows:&Rows,schema:&Schema,viewer:&str)->Option<Value>{Some(match e{
    Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="viewer"=>Value::Id(viewer.into()),
    Expr::Literal{value,..}=>match value{Literal::Str(s)=>Value::String(s.clone()),Literal::Bool(v)=>Value::Bool(*v),Literal::Null=>Value::Null,Literal::Number(s)=>Value::Int(s.parse().ok()?)},
    Expr::Focal{..}=>row.clone(),
    Expr::Member{base,name,..} if matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal{..})=>row.field(name).ok()?.clone(),
    Expr::Unary{op,value:x,..} if op=="not"=>Value::Bool(!boolean(value(x,row,rows,schema,viewer))?),
    Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..} if op=="and"||op=="or"=>{
        let a=boolean(value(left,row,rows,schema,viewer));let b=boolean(value(right,row,rows,schema,viewer));
        let and=op=="and";let decisive=!and;
        Value::Bool(if a==Some(decisive)||b==Some(decisive){decisive}else if a.is_some()&&b.is_some(){and}else{return None;})
    },
    Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=>{
        let a=value(left,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;let b=value(right,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;
        if matches!(op.as_str(),"+"|"-"|"*") {
            let (Value::Int(a),Value::Int(b))=(a,b)else{return None;};
            let n=match op.as_str(){"+"=>i128::from(a)+i128::from(b),"-"=>i128::from(a)-i128::from(b),_=>i128::from(a)*i128::from(b)};
            return Value::int(n).ok();
        }
        // The binder contextually types principal/id literals. This partial
        // evaluator refuses that coercion rather than using host strings.
        if !matches!((&a,&b),(Value::Id(_),Value::Id(_))|(Value::String(_),Value::String(_))|(Value::Int(_),Value::Int(_))|(Value::Bool(_),Value::Bool(_))|(Value::Null,_)|(_,Value::Null)){return None;}
        Value::Bool(match op.as_str(){"="=>a==b,"!="=>a!=b,"<"|"<="|">"|">="=>if let (Value::Int(a),Value::Int(b))=(a,b){match op.as_str(){"<"=>a<b,"<="=>a<=b,">"=>a>b,_=>a>=b}}else{return None;},_=>return None})
    },
    Expr::Call{name,args,..} if (name=="count"||name=="len")&&args.len()==1 => {
        let Value::Array(values)=value(&args[0],row,rows,schema,viewer)?else{return None;};
        Value::Int(values.len().try_into().ok()?)
    },
    Expr::Call{name,args,..} if name=="sum"&&args.len()==2 => {
        let Value::Array(values)=value(&args[0],row,rows,schema,viewer)?else{return None;};
        let sum=values.iter().try_fold(0i128,|sum,r|match value(&args[1],r,rows,schema,viewer)? {Value::Int(n)=>sum.checked_add(i128::from(n)),_=>None})?;
        Value::int(sum).ok()?
    },
    Expr::Array{items,..}=>Value::Array(items.iter().map(|e|value(e,row,rows,schema,viewer)).collect::<Option<_>>()?),
    Expr::Read(read)|Expr::Exists{read,..}=>{
        let t=schema.table(&read.table)?;
        if t.is_expiring()||t.not_before.is_some()||t.through.is_some()||schema.maintain_for(&read.table).is_some()||read.filter.is_some()||read.range.is_some()||!read.seeks.is_empty()||read.matching.is_some(){return None;}
        let mut pins=Vec::new();
        for key in &read.keys{let column=key.column.as_ref()?;let val=value(&key.value,row,rows,schema,viewer)?;
            let kind=t.columns.get(column)?;
            use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType as C;
            let typed=match (kind.required(),&val) {
                (_,Value::Null)=>kind.is_optional(),
                (C::Id|C::Principal|C::Ref{..},Value::Id(_))=>true,
                (C::Text{..},Value::String(_))=>true,
                (C::Enum{variants},Value::String(v))=>variants.contains(v),
                (C::Int|C::IntRange{..}|C::Time,Value::Int(_))=>true,
                (C::Bool,Value::Bool(_))=>true,_=>false,
            };
            if !typed{return None;}pins.push((column,val));}
        // Integrity assertions quantify over the complete raw snapshot, not
        // a caller-filtered program population. Temporal/captured populations
        // above remain unsupported. No solver proof uses these samples.
        let selected=rows.get(&read.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|pins.iter().all(|(c,v)|r.field(c).ok()==Some(v))).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if matches!(e,Expr::Exists{..}) {Value::Bool(!selected.is_empty())}else{Value::Array(selected)}
    },
    _=>return None,
})}
//! Equal-image substitution only for engine-enforced immutable local fields.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#111-avoid-duplicate-concrete-searches — no reference or mutable-field equality is assumed.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::schema::{Table,Constraint};

pub(super) fn stable(table:&Table,rule:&RuleExpr)->RuleExpr{
    fn term(table:&Table,value:&mut RuleTerm){
        if let RuleTerm::OldField(field)=value && (field=="id"||table.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(field)))) {
            *value=RuleTerm::Field(field.clone());
        }
    }
    fn walk(table:&Table,rule:&mut RuleExpr){match rule{
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)|RuleExpr::Compare(_,a,b)=>{term(table,a);term(table,b);},
        RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..}|RuleExpr::Created{key,equalities,..}=>{
            for value in key.iter_mut().chain(equalities.iter_mut().map(|(_,t)|t)){term(table,value);}
        },
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for rule in xs{walk(table,rule)},
        RuleExpr::Not(rule)=>walk(table,rule),
        RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Deny|RuleExpr::Public{..}=>{},
    }}
    let mut out=rule.clone();walk(table,&mut out);out
}
pub(super) fn update(table:&Table,policy:&RuleExpr)->Result<bool,String>{
    kernel::implies(&stable(table,&table.update_rule_judged()),&stable(table,&policy.old_and_next()))
}

//! A campaign fork retains activated semantics without running adoption again.
use super::*;
impl Engine {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — isolation includes entropy, assets and ephemeral ownership.
    pub fn fork_fixture(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        if self.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.ephemeral.is_some()) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture fork needs an isolated ephemeral-hub snapshot".into()));
        }
        if self.presence.is_some(){return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("fixture fork needs an isolated external presence snapshot".into()));}
        let store=self.store.fork_memory()?;
        let identity=store.meta("store_id")?.ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Store("fixture store has no identity".into()))?;
        Ok(Self {
            timeline_schedule:timeline::Schedule::default(),
            ephemeral:std::sync::Arc::new(ephemeral::Hub::new(identity,self.generation,&self.schema)),
            store,schema:self.schema.clone(),programs:self.programs.clone(),ceilings:self.ceilings,
            entropy:crate::ids::seed(),minter:crate::ids::Mint::default(),generation:self.generation,
            expiry_after:self.expiry_after.clone(),dev_effects:self.dev_effects,
            #[cfg(feature="native")] native:self.native.clone(),
            #[cfg(feature="native")] native_logs:native::Logs::default(),presence:None,
        })
    }
}

impl Engine {
    /// Judge independent fixture intent with the engine's authorized rule hops.
    pub fn fixture_policy(&mut self,rule:&crate::schema::RuleExpr,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,next:&Value,now:i64)->Result<rules::Verdict,RuntimeError>{
        let tx=self.store.read_transaction()?;
        let context=if viewer==crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL{Context::system()}else{Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new())};
        let mut runner=Runner::new(&tx,&self.schema,context.at(now),Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(),Cut::Abort),0);
        let bytes=runner.rule_scratch(rule);
        runner.authorized_scratch(bytes,&[],|runner,allowance|{
            let mut probe=read::RuleProbe{runner,allowance};
            rules::evaluate_verdict(rule,viewer,old,next,false,&mut probe)
        })
    }
}
//! Isolated complete fixtures, including captured authority and asset ownership.
use super::*;
impl Store {
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — fork committed state, never reinterpret stored rows as new seed input.
    pub fn fork_memory(&self) -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        let mut fork = Self::memory()?;
        {
            let backup = rusqlite::backup::Backup::new(&self.conn, &mut fork.conn)?;
            backup.run_to_completion(256, std::time::Duration::ZERO, None)?;
        }
        fork.catalog = self.catalog.clone();
        fork.identity = self.identity.clone();
        fork.server = self.server;
        if self.assets_dir.exists() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(&fork.assets_dir).map_err(io)?;
            for entry in std::fs::read_dir(&self.assets_dir).map_err(io)? {
                let entry = entry.map_err(io)?;
                if !entry.file_type().map_err(io)?.is_file() {
                    return Err(RuntimeError::Store("fixture asset ledger contains a non-file".into()));
                }
                std::fs::copy(entry.path(), fork.assets_dir.join(entry.file_name())).map_err(io)?;
            }
        }
        Ok(fork)
    }
}

fn io(e:std::io::Error)->RuntimeError {RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string())}

impl Store {
    /// Independent valid worlds share verified bytes, never source row state.
    pub fn empty_with_asset_ledger(&self)->Result<Self,RuntimeError>{
        let fresh=Self::memory()?;
        let ids=self.conn.prepare("SELECT id FROM _assets")?.query_map([],|r|r.get::<_,String>(0))?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
        for id in ids{
            let mut asset=self.asset(&id)?.ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Store("fixture asset disappeared".into()))?;
            asset.home=None;asset.released_at=None;
            fresh.record_asset(&asset)?;
        }
        if self.assets_dir.exists(){
            std::fs::create_dir_all(&fresh.assets_dir).map_err(io)?;
            for entry in std::fs::read_dir(&self.assets_dir).map_err(io)?{
                let entry=entry.map_err(io)?;
                if !entry.file_type().map_err(io)?.is_file(){return Err(RuntimeError::Store("fixture asset is not a regular file".into()));}
                std::fs::copy(entry.path(),fresh.assets_dir.join(entry.file_name())).map_err(io)?;
            }
        }
        Ok(fresh)
    }
}
//! CLI security reporting; compile/test evidence share one analyzer.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#5-exposure-report — full audit is opt-in, intent notes are on ordinary check.
use super::*;
use snapback4_lang::security::{self,Report};
mod http;
pub(super) fn unauthenticated(bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)])->bool {
    match http::run(bound,sources){Ok(_)=>true,Err(why)=>{eprintln!("security HTTP failure: {why}");false}}
}
pub(super) fn report(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,options:&Options)->Report{
    let mut report=security::analyze(bound);
    if let Ok(sources)=project.sources(){security::add_source_notes(&mut report,&sources,bound);}
    let mut campaign=security::challenges::Campaign::default();
    if !bound.invariants.is_empty(){
        let mut selection=options.clone();
        if !selection.flags.contains_key("fixture")&&project.root.join("fixtures").is_dir(){selection.flags.insert("fixture".into(),project.root.join("fixtures").to_string_lossy().into_owned());}
        if selection.flags.contains_key("fixture"){
            match super::test_fixture::engine_at(project,bound,&selection,1_800_000_000_000){
                Ok(fixture)=>security::challenges::fixture_observers(bound,&fixture,&mut campaign),
                Err(why)=>for verdict in &mut report.invariants{verdict.reasons.push(format!("fixture reachability not covered: {why}"));},
            }
        }else{for verdict in &mut report.invariants{verdict.reasons.push("no trusted fixture: application reachability not covered; seed/synthetic witnesses are non-failing".into());}}
    }
    let sites=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Unproved&&!campaign.violations.iter().any(|c|c.invariant_site==v.invariant.site.to_string()&&c.reachability==security::challenges::Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace)).map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect();
    let campaign=security::challenges::run_selected_after(bound,Some(&sites),campaign);
    security::challenges::apply(&mut report,&campaign);report
}
pub fn audit(options:&Options)->i32{
    let project=project_or_exit();
    let bound=compiled_or_exit(match project.compile(){Ok(b)=>b,Err(e)=>{eprintln!("audit: {e}");return 2;}},options.json);
    let report=report(&project,&bound,options);
    if options.json {println!("{}",serde_json::to_string(&report).unwrap());}
    else {print!("{}",report.text_with_all(options.flags.contains_key("all")));}
    if report.refuted(){1}else{0}
}
pub(super) fn summary(report:&Report,all:bool){
    for v in &report.invariants {if !all&&v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability!=security::challenges::Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace){eprintln!("security: {} refuted by seed/synthetic witness only (non-failing; --all for detail)",v.invariant.target_label());continue;}eprintln!("security {:?}: {} {}",v.status,v.invariant.target_label(),v.invariant.predicate);for why in &v.reasons{eprintln!("  {why}");}if let Some(c)=&v.counterexample{eprintln!("  counterexample {}",serde_json::to_string(c).unwrap());}}
    for note in &report.notes{eprintln!("{} {}: {} — {}",note.site,note.code,note.message,note.rewrite);}
    if !report.invariants.is_empty(){eprintln!("security: audit --json lists paths, exclusions and grant writers; guide security explains proof scope");}
}

//! Field writes use the old owner; row transition rules still constrain them.
// @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — ownership grants only the explicitly touched owned fields.
use super::runner::Runner;
use super::rules::{self, Probe};
use crate::{error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError}, schema::{Effect, RuleExpr}, value::Value};

/// Delivery accounting must not make another recipient's unknown membership
/// a dependency of the writer's prediction. Server witnesses remain exact.
struct DeliveryProbe<'a, 'b> {
    runner: &'a mut Runner<'b>,
    known: bool,
    allowance: &'a super::Authorization,
}

impl Probe for DeliveryProbe<'_, '_> {
    fn take_changes(&mut self) -> Option<Vec<(i64, super::rules::Verdict)>> { self.runner.witness_changes.take() }
    fn replace_expiry(&mut self, expiry: Option<i64>) -> Option<i64> { self.runner.next_expiry.replace(expiry) }
    fn exists(&mut self, table: &str, index: &str, key: &[Value], equalities: &[(String, Value)]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        if !self.runner.read_charge(MeterKind::RuleProbes, 1)? { return Ok(false); }
        let (present, known) = self.runner.probe_witness(self.allowance, table, index, key, equalities, false)?;
        self.known &= known;
        Ok(present)
    }

    fn created(&mut self, _table: &str, _index: &str, _key: &[Value]) -> bool { false }
}

impl<'r> Runner<'r> {
    pub(crate) fn field_owner_can_read(&mut self, rule: &RuleExpr, owner: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        // Recipient witnesses are exact but are not the caller's observations.
        // Partition accounting prepays their schema-derived probe/step bound.
        // A recipient's witness deadline never enters the writer's answer: the scope is dropped, not emitted.
        let delivery_expiry = self.next_expiry.scope();
        let result = self.authorized_scratch(self.rule_scratch(rule), &[], |runner, allowance| {
            let mut probe = DeliveryProbe { runner, known: true, allowance };
            let readable = rules::evaluate(rule, owner, None, row, false, &mut probe)?;
            Ok(readable && probe.known)
        });
        drop(delivery_expiry);
        result
    }

    pub(crate) fn prepay_field_delivery(&mut self, rule: &RuleExpr, slots: u64, maintained: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let probes = slots.saturating_mul(rules::rule_probes(rule));
        if maintained {
            self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::RuleProbes, probes)?;
            self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Steps, slots)?;
        } else {
            self.meter.charge(MeterKind::RuleProbes, probes)?;
            self.meter.step(slots)?;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub(super) fn authorize_fields(&self, table: &str, row: &Value, supplied: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
        let definition = &self.schema.tables[table];
        let fields = supplied.as_object()?;
        let mut owned = false;
        let mut ordinary = false;
        for field in fields.keys().filter(|field| field.as_str() != "id") {
            if let Some(authority) = definition.written_by.get(field) {
                owned = true;
                if !self.context.is_system() && row.field(&authority.owner).ok() != Some(&Value::Id(self.context.viewer.clone())) {
                    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
                    self.refusing_clause(table, &format!("field authority {field}"));
                    return Err(RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_FIELD_AUTHORITY", message: format!("{table}.{field} is written by .{}; omit {field} or write as the principal in .{} (guide rules)", authority.owner, authority.owner) });
                }
            } else { ordinary = true; }
        }
        Ok(owned && !ordinary)
    }

    pub(super) fn authorize_update_fields(&mut self, table: &str, old: &Value, next: &Value, patch: &Value, readable: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        let owned_only = self.authorize_fields(table, old, patch).map_err(|error| if readable { error } else { self.no_write_target() })?;
        // An unrelated owner's omitted field is unknown, not its literal
        // default. It prevents prediction only when this transition needs it,
        // or when changing ownership would expose it to this device.
        let definition = &self.schema.tables[table];
        if self.device && definition.written_by.iter().any(|(field, authority)|
            !authority.audience && old.field(&authority.owner).ok() != Some(&Value::Id(self.context.viewer.clone()))
            && ((!owned_only || definition.update_transition) && definition.rules.update.needs_field_value(field, &definition.columns[field])
                || next.field(&authority.owner).ok() == Some(&Value::Id(self.context.viewer.clone())))) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction(format!("{table}: this transition needs another principal's private field; queue it for the server (guide rules)")));
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — a named transition is not permission to test guesses against someone else's private value.
        if !self.context.is_system() && let Some((field, authority)) = definition.written_by.iter().find(|(field, authority)|
            !authority.audience && old.field(&authority.owner).ok() != Some(&Value::Id(self.context.viewer.clone()))
            && definition.rules.update.needs_field_value(field, &definition.columns[*field])) {
            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
            self.refusing_clause(table, &format!("field authority {field}"));
            return Err(if readable { RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_FIELD_AUTHORITY", message: format!("{table}.{field}: this transition needs the private value written by .{}; write as that owner or declare readable by audience (guide rules)", authority.owner) } } else { self.no_write_target() });
        }
        let transition = self.schema.tables[table].update_transition;
        if (!owned_only || transition) && !self.check_rule(table, Effect::Update, Some(old), next)? {
            return Err(if readable { RuntimeError::Rule { table: table.into(), effect: Effect::Update } } else { self.no_write_target() });
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub(crate) fn disclosed_row<'a>(&self, definition: &'a crate::schema::Table, row: Value) -> crate::value::Disclosed<'a> {
        match self.system_access() {
            Some(system) => definition.system_fields(row, &system),
            None => definition.project_fields(row, Some(&self.context.viewer)),
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn project_row(&mut self, table: &str, row: Value) -> Result<super::authorized_point::AuthorizedRow<'r>, RuntimeError> {
        let definition = &self.schema.tables[table];
        // A merge may retain previously authorized results after its next lane
        // cuts. Revalidate their authority internally without treating that
        // existing logical cut as a denied row. Preserve the original cut.
        let cap = self.meter.capped_at.take();
        let result = self.authorized_scratch(self.rule_scratch(&definition.rules.read), &[], |runner, _| runner.authorize_image(table,row));
        if cap.is_some() { self.meter.capped_at = cap; }
        result?.ok_or_else(|| { self.refusing_clause(table, "read rule"); self.no_write_target() })
    }

    pub(super) fn private_scan_authority(&self, table: &str, uniform: bool, admitted: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        // A bounded hidden-row walk exposes population through both its meter
        // and its cut. Require a readable scope before walking protected rows.
        if !uniform && !admitted && self.schema.tables[table].written_by.values().any(|a| !a.audience) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_FIELD_AUTHORITY", message: format!("{table}: scope this read to a uniformly readable audience before scanning rows with private fields (guide rules)") });
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

//! Which row images an update rule judges.
// @ref LLP 3000.007#8 — item 7(a): an unnamed `update <- R` judges the row before and after the write, `R[old] and R[next]`.
//
// The rules card and `why NOT_FOUND` say "the update rule judges old and
// next", as Postgres applies an `UPDATE` policy's `USING` to the old row and
// (absent `WITH CHECK`) to the new one. Judging only the next image let any
// caller take over another principal's row under `update <- .owner = viewer`
// by writing `owner: viewer` (found by the Lean oracle, LLP 3000.035 §5.2).
use super::{RuleExpr, RuleTerm, Table};

impl RuleExpr {
    fn terms_mut(&mut self, term: &mut dyn FnMut(&mut RuleTerm)) {
        match self {
            Self::Eq(a, b) | Self::Ne(a, b) | Self::Compare(_, a, b) => { term(a); term(b); }
            Self::And(items) | Self::Or(items) => for item in items { item.terms_mut(term); },
            Self::Not(inner) => inner.terms_mut(term),
            Self::Exists { key, equalities, .. } | Self::Created { key, equalities, .. } =>
                key.iter_mut().chain(equalities.iter_mut().map(|(_, value)| value)).for_each(term),
            Self::Public { .. } | Self::Allow | Self::Deny => {}
        }
    }

    /// An unnamed update rule's meaning: `R[old] and R[next]`, old first.
    /// In the old copy `.f` becomes `old.f` and reference hops start from the
    /// old row; literals and `viewer` are unchanged.
    pub fn old_and_next(&self) -> RuleExpr {
        let mut old = self.clone();
        old.terms_mut(&mut |term| match term {
            RuleTerm::Field(field) => *term = RuleTerm::OldField(std::mem::take(field)),
            RuleTerm::Reference { old, .. } | RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { old, .. } => *old = true,
            _ => {}
        });
        Self::And(vec![old, self.clone()])
    }
}

impl Table {
    /// Whether the engine judges this table's update rule over the old image
    /// as well: true for an unnamed rule that reads a column a patch can
    /// change. A named `update (old, next)` transition is judged as written.
    /// A rule reading only `id` and immutable columns is judged once: a patch
    /// cannot change those inputs (a hidden target drops immutable patch
    /// fields; a readable mismatch is refused), so both images give one
    /// verdict at one prepaid probe cost. The decision is static, so the
    /// meter never depends on the row.
    pub fn judges_old_image(&self) -> bool {
        !self.update_transition && self.rules.update.any_term(&|term| matches!(term,
            RuleTerm::Field(column) | RuleTerm::Reference { column, .. } | RuleTerm::ReferenceChain { column, .. }
                if column != "id" && !self.constraints.iter().any(|constraint| matches!(constraint, super::Constraint::Immutable { fields } if fields.contains(column)))))
    }

    /// The update rule exactly as the engine evaluates it, for cost reports.
    pub fn update_rule_judged(&self) -> std::borrow::Cow<'_, RuleExpr> {
        if self.judges_old_image() { std::borrow::Cow::Owned(self.rules.update.old_and_next()) } else { std::borrow::Cow::Borrowed(&self.rules.update) }
    }
}

pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> Vec<Invariant> {
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for decl in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d| &d.invariants) {
        let Some(info)=catalog.tables.get(&decl.table) else {
            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site,"E_SECURITY_TARGET",format!("invariant names unknown table {}",decl.table),"declare the table or correct its name").guide("security")); continue;
        };
        let named_operation=operation::resolve(catalog,decl);
        if (!matches!(decl.target.as_str(),"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") && named_operation.is_none())
            || decl.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"&&!info.columns.contains_key(c)) {
            diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&decl.site,"E_SECURITY_TARGET","invariant needs a declared column or table effect","use invariant table.read only <policy>, table.column write only <policy>, or table always <predicate>").guide("security")); continue;
        }
        let effect=match decl.target.as_str(){"insert"=>ast::Effect::Insert,"update"|"write"=>ast::Effect::Update,"delete"=>ast::Effect::Delete,_=>ast::Effect::Read};
        // Independent intent may use authorized reference operands even when a
        // runtime read gate must use captured authority. It never installs a gate.
        let effect=if effect==ast::Effect::Read{ast::Effect::Insert}else{effect};
        let rule=ast::RuleDecl{effect,bindings:Vec::new(),value:decl.value.clone(),site:decl.site.clone()};
        let (policy,unsupported)=match super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&rule) {
            Ok(rule)=>(Some(rule),None),Err(error)=>match temporal::bind(catalog,info,&decl.value) {
                Some(rule) if decl.target=="read" => (Some(rule),None),
                _ => (None,Some(format!("policy outside rule proof fragment: {} ({})",error.message,error.code))),
            },
        };
        let requirements=named_operation.as_ref().map(|(_,p)| operation::requirements(catalog,info,p)).unwrap_or_default();
        out.push(Invariant{operation:named_operation.map(|(name,_)|name),requirements,syntax:Some(decl.value.clone()),table:decl.table.clone(),target:decl.target.clone(),column:decl.column.clone(),predicate:crate::print::expression(&decl.value),site:decl.site.clone(),policy,unsupported});
    }
    out
}

            for reason in &v.reasons {out.push_str(&format!("    {reason}\n"));}
            for o in &v.obligations {out.push_str(&format!("    {:?}: {} — {}\n",o.status,o.path,o.reason));if let Some(repair)=&o.repair{out.push_str(&format!("      repair: {repair}\n"));}}
            if let Some(c)=&v.counterexample {out.push_str(&format!("    counterexample {}\n",serde_json::to_string(c).unwrap()));}
        }
        for note in &self.notes {out.push_str(&format!("  {} {}: {}\n    {}\n",note.site,note.code,note.message,note.rewrite));}
        for (role,entries) in &self.roles {

        }

            for (column,audience) in &x.column_audiences {out.push_str(&format!("  column {column} audience: {audience}\n"));}
            for path in &x.paths {out.push_str(&format!("  {path}\n"));}

        }

        out.push_str(&format!("  exclusions: {}\n",self.exclusions.join("; ")));
        out.push_str("exposures without an invariant (conditional role/row coverage; declarations are not proof):\n");
        for x in &self.uncovered {out.push_str(&format!("  {}.{}: {}; rows: {}\n",x.table,x.effect,x.audience,x.row_predicate));}
        if self.uncovered.is_empty(){out.push_str("  none\n");}out
    }

/// Analyze declarations without using fixtures as proof assumptions.
pub fn analyze(bound:&Bound)->Report {
    let exposures=audit::exposures(bound);
    let mut verdicts=Vec::new();
    for inv in &bound.invariants {
        let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
        let mut reasons=Vec::new();let mut direct=Status::Proved;
        if let Some(why)=&inv.unsupported {reasons.push(why.clone());direct=Status::Unproved;}

            // including seed/adoption, with no assumption about a writer.

                RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null))|
                RuleExpr::Ne(RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null),RuleTerm::Field(f)) =>
                    table.columns.get(f).is_some_and(|t| !t.is_optional() && !matches!(t,snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Json{..}) && !table.engine_field(f)),
                _=>false,
            };
            if !nonnull {direct=Status::Unproved; reasons.push("row assertion is not implied by the implemented required-column theorem; transitions, counts and time require an additional proof".into());}
        } else if inv.operation.is_some() {
            match operation::prove(bound,inv) {Ok(true)=>{},Ok(false)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push("operation admission does not imply the policy; an actual operation challenge is required".into());},Err(e)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(e);}}
        } else {
            for word in effects(inv) {
                let admission=if word=="update" {table.update_rule_judged().into_owned()}else if word=="read" {columns::admission(table,inv.column.as_deref())}else{table.rules.for_effect(effect(word)).clone()};
                let implication=if word=="update"{images::update(table,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())}else{kernel::implies(&admission,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())};
                match implication {
                    Ok(true)=>{},Ok(false)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(format!("{word} admission does not imply intent over independent predicates; a concrete replay is required for refutation"));},
                    Err(why)=>{direct=Status::Unproved;reasons.push(format!("{word}: {why}"));},
                }
                // Update implication judges both images. Only id/explicit
