//! Real operation histories from a complete trusted fixture, including assets.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — only actual fixture traces establish application reachability.
use super::*;
const LIMIT:usize=512;
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Step {pub operation:String,pub viewer:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub result:serde_json::Value}

pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into(),"account:security-no-role".into(),"service:security".into()]);
    out.extend(bound.personas.iter().map(|p|if p.contains(':'){p.clone()}else{format!("dev:{p}")}));
    fn seats(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value{
        serde_json::Value::String(s) if s.starts_with("service:")&&s.len()>8=>{out.insert(s.clone());},
        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{seats(x,out)},
        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).unwrap(),&mut out);
    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&mut out);}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).unwrap(),&mut out);
    out.into_iter().collect()
}
fn actors(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for row in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{
        if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!out.contains(p){out.push(p.clone());}
    }}}
    out
}
pub(super) fn fixture_cases(args:&BTreeMap<String,ir::ArgType>,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    fn values(name:&str,k:&ir::ArgType,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String])->Option<Vec<Value>>{
        Some(match k{
            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|s|Value::String(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
            ir::ArgType::Time=>vec![Value::Int(NOW-86_400_000),Value::Int(NOW),Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000)],
            ir::ArgType::Principal=>actors.iter().map(|s|Value::Id(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rows.get(table).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())=>rows[table].iter().filter_map(|r|r.field("id").ok().cloned()).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{let mut v=vec![Value::Null];v.extend(values(name,inner,rows,actors)?);v},
            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
        })
    }
    let mut cases=vec![BTreeMap::new()];
    for (name,k) in args{
        let Some(vals)=values(name,k,rows,actors)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history needs a typed asset argument"));return Vec::new();};
        let mut next=Vec::new();
        for n in 0..vals.len() {for c in &cases{
            let mut case=c.clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[n%vals.len()].clone());
            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{break;}
        }if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break;}}
        cases=next;
    }cases
}
fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str){
    let committed=|steps:&[Step]|steps.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&committed(&v.history)==committed(steps)){return;}
    out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace,observations:None,history:steps.to_vec(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:steps.last().map(|s|s.operation.clone()).unwrap_or("$fixture.read".into()),effect:effect.into(),row:row.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:initial.clone(),rule_path:"actual authored operation/read from the trusted fixture".into(),scope:"reachable from recorded trusted fixture; finite trace, not an all-history proof".into()});
}
fn typed_disclosure(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value)->bool{
    use crate::types::Ty;
    let payload=|data:&Value|matches!(data,Value::Row(_))&&data.field("id").ok()==Some(key)&&column.is_none_or(|c|data.field(c).ok().is_some_and(|v|physical.field(c).ok()==Some(v)));
    match ty{
        Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==table=>payload(data),
        Ty::Optional(inner)=>typed_disclosure(inner,data,table,key,column,physical),
        Ty::Page(t,_) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(payload)),
        Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|typed_disclosure(inner,r,table,key,column,physical))),
        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(name,ty)|data.field(name).ok().is_some_and(|v|typed_disclosure(ty,v,table,key,column,physical))),
        _=>false,
    }
}
fn typed_source(ty:&crate::types::Ty,table:&str)->bool{
    use crate::types::Ty;
    match ty{Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t)|Ty::Page(t,_)=>t==table,Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>typed_source(inner,table),Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.values().any(|ty|typed_source(ty,table)),_=>false}
}
fn observer_tables(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([table.into()]);
    loop{let before=out.len();
        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if out.contains(&m.source){out.insert(m.target.clone());if let Some(owner)=&m.private{out.insert(owner.table.clone());}}}
        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|out.contains(&edge.parent))||[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|t|out.contains(t))){out.insert(name.clone());}}
        if out.len()==before{return out;}
    }
}
fn reserve_observers(out:&mut Campaign,calls:usize)->bool{
    if out.history_observer_calls+calls>32768{out.unsupported.insert("history read observers capped at 32768 calls; remaining disclosure combinations not covered".into());false}else{out.history_observer_calls+=calls;true}
}
fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    if !reserve_observers(out,0)||out.history_observer_calls>=32768{return;}
    let queries=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!bp.program.native).filter(|bp|{
        let json=serde_json::to_string(&bp.program).unwrap();
        if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|tag|json.contains(tag)){
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque history observer needs disclosure provenance",bp.program.name));false
        }else{true}
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"){
        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true));
        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}

        let observing=observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
            let committed=steps.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count();
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&v.history.iter().filter(|s|s.result.get("state").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some("sent")).count()==committed){continue;}
            match policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row,e){Ok(false)=>{},Ok(true)=>continue,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {why}",inv.target_label()));continue;}}
            let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
            if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].written_by.get(c)).is_some_and(|gate|!gate.audience&&row.field(&gate.owner).ok()!=Some(&Value::Id(viewer.clone()))){continue 'actors;}

            // A typed authored row return is already a payload witness. It does
            // not require an invalid absent world that deletes referenced data.
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|typed_source(&p.result,&inv.table)){
                let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program.args,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read-observer arguments capped at 32",bp.program.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&bp.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",bp.program.name));continue;}};
                    out.attempted+=1;
                    if let Ok(q)=e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW))&&q.complete&&!q.unavailable&&typed_disclosure(&bp.result,&q.data,&inv.table,key,inv.column.as_deref(),row){
                        out.attempted+=1;
                        if !matches!(e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)),Ok(again) if again.complete&&!again.unavailable&&again.data==q.data){
                            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read payload is not repeatable on the committed image",bp.program.name));continue;
                        }
                        violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,if inv.column.is_some(){"column read observation"}else{"read"});
                        if let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=bp.program.name.clone();v.args=args;v.rule_path="authored query repeatably returned a compiler-typed row/column payload of the protected table while current policy is false".into();}
                        continue 'actors;
                    }
                }
            }
            if inv.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|c!="id"){continue 'actors;}
            if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|t.columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Ref{table} if table==&inv.table))){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history paired absence needs a referentially valid closure",inv.table));continue;}
            let Ok(id)=key.as_str()else{continue;};
            let mut absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world fork: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world refused: {why}",inv.table));continue;}
            let absent_rows=match readback(&absent){Ok(r)=>r,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: absent world readback: {why}",inv.table));continue;}};
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&absent_rows,viewer,None,row,&mut absent)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|observing.iter().any(|t|super::super::audit::mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),t))){
                let p=&bp.program;
                let cases=fixture_cases(&p.args,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: read-observer arguments capped at 32; remaining combinations not covered",p.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,4){return;}
                    let raw_args=args;
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&p.args,&raw_args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
                    if args!=raw_args{
                        absent=match fork(bound,e,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger fork: {why}",p.name));continue;}};
                        if let Err(why)=absent.seed_delete_at(&inv.table,id,NOW){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired history argument ledger absence: {why}",p.name));continue;}
                        if readback(&absent)!=Ok(absent_rows.clone()){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: paired argument ledger changes the absent base world",p.name));continue;}
                    }
                    out.attempted+=2;
                    let (Ok(a),Ok(b))=(paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone()))else{continue;};
                    if a==b{continue;}
                    if !matches!((paired::observe(e,p,viewer,args.clone()),paired::observe(&mut absent,p,viewer,args.clone())),(Ok(aa),Ok(bb)) if a==aa&&b==bb){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: paired history observation is unstable or unavailable on repeat",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;}
                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,"read");
                    if let Some(v)=out.violations.last_mut(){v.operation=p.name.clone();v.args=args;v.observations=Some(paired::Observations{present:a,absent:b,absent_rows:absent_rows.clone()});}
                    continue 'actors;
                }
            }
        }}
    }
}
fn prior_policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,prior:&mut Option<Engine>)->Result<bool,String>{
    match prior.as_mut(){Some(e)=>policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row,e),None=>policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row)}
}
fn assertions(bound:&Bound,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
            if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            // A pre-existing seed defect is supplemental; a newly violated
            // assertion or changed image is an authored committed observation.
            if old!=Some(row)||old.is_some_and(|r|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(r),r,prior)==Ok(true)){
                violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,"always");
            }
        }
    }
}
fn changed(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,before:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,after:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,steps:&[Step],prior:&mut Option<Engine>,current:&mut Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    assertions(bound,before,after,initial,viewer,steps,prior,current,out);
    if !super::super::census::body(&p.body){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: loop/opaque write boundary requires executed-effect tracing",p.name));return;}
    for inv in &bound.invariants{

        let judged;
        let inv=if inv.target=="always"{inv}else{
            let Some(stable)=stability::necessary(bound,p,inv)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}: unknown/implicit policy changes need executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.target_label()));continue;};
            judged=stable;&judged

exec

 succeeded in 0ms:
        };
        // A net image cannot identify an intermediate write or implicit effect.
        let writes=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if writes.len()!=1 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{} / {}.{}: multiple or implicit writes require executed-effect tracing",p.name,inv.table,inv.target));continue;}
        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1!="delete"&&before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: commit-end/implicit retirement is not an executed delete",p.name));}
        if (inv.target=="delete"||inv.operation.is_some())&&writes[0].1=="delete"{
            for old in before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|old|!bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].expired(old,NOW)&&!after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok())){

                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: delete policy dependency changes before disclosure",p.name));continue;}
                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,old,Some(old),initial,"delete");}
            }
        }
        for row in after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let old=before.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok()&&!bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].expired(r,NOW));
            if old==Some(row){continue;}
            let effect=if old.is_some(){"update"}else{"insert"};
            if !super::super::effects(inv).contains(&effect){continue;}
            if inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|old.is_some_and(|o|o.field(c).ok()==row.field(c).ok())){continue;}
            if inv.target=="always" {
                if policy_in(bound,inv,after,viewer,old,row,current)==Ok(false){violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);}
            }else{
                let deps=inv.policy.as_ref().map(kernel::dependencies).unwrap_or_default();
                if ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,_)|deps.contains(t)&&t!=&inv.table) {out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: write policy dependency changes within the operation; tracing required",p.name));continue;}
                if prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,old,row,prior)==Ok(false)||old.is_some_and(|old|prior_policy(bound,inv,before,viewer,Some(old),old,prior)==Ok(false)){
                    violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,old,initial,effect);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
fn role_candidates<'a>(bound:&Bound,p:&'a Program,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],cases:&[BTreeMap<String,Value>],fixture:&Engine)->Vec<(&'a Program,String,BTreeMap<String,Value>)>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut groups=Vec::new();
    let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
    let Ok(mut judge)=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())else{return out;};
    for (table,_) in ir::write_tables(p){
        let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&table);
        if !columns.iter().any(|c|json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))){continue;}
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
        for row in initial.get(&table).into_iter().flatten(){
            let principal=t.columns.iter().find_map(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal).then(||row.field(c).ok()).flatten());
            let Some(principal)=principal else{continue;};
            let mut targets=Vec::new();
            for case in cases{
                let mut args=case.clone();
                for (name,kind) in &p.args{match kind{
                    ir::ArgType::Principal=>{args.insert(name.clone(),principal.clone());},
                    ir::ArgType::Ref{table:target} if target==&table=>{if let Ok(id)=row.field("id"){args.insert(name.clone(),id.clone());}},_=>{}
                }}
                if !targets.contains(&args){targets.push(args);}
            }
            let mut row_cases=Vec::new();
            for actor in actors{
                let owner=columns.iter().filter_map(|c|t.written_by.get(c)).any(|a|row.field(&a.owner).ok()==Some(&Value::Id(actor.clone())));
                let gate=judge.fixture_policy(&t.update_rule_judged(),actor,Some(row),row,NOW).ok();
                if !owner&&gate!=Some(rules::Verdict::Readable){continue;}
                for args in &targets{row_cases.push((p,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
            }
            groups.push(row_cases);
        }
    }
    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(case)=g.get(n){out.push(case.clone());}}}
    out
}
pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    if bound.invariants.is_empty(){return;}
    let initial=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    if initial.values().all(Vec::is_empty){out.unsupported.insert("history needs a trusted fixture; generated snapshots have separate reachability labels".into());return;}
    if initial.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{out.unsupported.insert("history fixture exceeds 1000 rows".into());return;}
    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
    let probes=Vec::new();
    let mut groups=Vec::new();
    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native){
        let cases=fixture_cases(&bp.program.args,&initial,&actors,out);
        let mut group=role_candidates(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,&cases,fixture);
        // Diagonal order gives both actors and argument values an early turn.
        for shift in 0..actors.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(actors.len()){
            if cases.is_empty(){break;}
            group.push((&bp.program,actors[(n+shift)%actors.len()].clone(),cases[n%cases.len()].clone()));
        }}
        if !group.is_empty(){groups.push(group);}
    }
    let mut candidates=Vec::new();
    for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for g in &groups{if let Some(c)=g.get(n){candidates.push(c.clone());}}}
    let mut base=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="always"){
        for row in initial.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){for viewer in &actors{
            if policy_in(bound,inv,&initial,viewer,None,row,&mut base)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            let mut witness=Campaign::default();violation(&mut witness,inv,&[],viewer,row,None,&initial,"always");
            if let Some(mut v)=witness.violations.pop(){v.reachability=Reachability::TrustedSeedOnly;v.operation="$fixture.assertion".into();v.rule_path="initial trusted fixture assertion; no authored mutation".into();v.scope="definite violation in trusted seed; no authored transition claimed; non-failing supplemental evidence".into();out.violations.push(v);}break;
        }}
    }
    observe(bound,&mut base,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
    // Spend half the pair budget on grant changes followed by an operation
    // as the affected principal; interleave first steps and successor names.
    let mut role_groups=Vec::new();
    for (i,(p,_,args)) in candidates.iter().enumerate().take(LIMIT){
        let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
        let rosters=ir::write_tables(p).into_iter().filter_map(|(t,_)|{
            let roles=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,&t);
            roles.iter().any(|c|json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))).then_some(t)
        }).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
        if rosters.is_empty(){continue;}
        let mut recipients=BTreeSet::new();
        for (name,kind) in &p.args {if let Some(value)=args.get(name){match kind {
            ir::ArgType::Principal=>{if let Value::Id(actor)=value{recipients.insert(actor.clone());}},
            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rosters.contains(table)=>{
                if let Some(row)=initial.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==Some(value)){
                    for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{if matches!(kind.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(actor))=row.field(column){recipients.insert(actor.clone());}}
                }
            },_=>{}
        }}}
        let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
        let successors=candidates.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(j,(next,actor,_))|(next.name!=p.name&&recipients.contains(actor)&&seen.insert((next.name.clone(),actor.clone()))).then_some((i,j))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if !successors.is_empty(){role_groups.push(successors);}
    }
    let mut role_pairs=Vec::new();
    'pairs:for n in 0..role_groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for group in &role_groups{if let Some(pair)=group.get(n){role_pairs.push(*pair);if role_pairs.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'pairs;}}}}
    // Breadth first, then targeted and diagonal pairs: no first step owns the cap.
    let singles=candidates.len().min(LIMIT);
    let mut setups=Vec::new();let mut role_changes=Vec::new();
    let mut setup_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut role_seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut pair_plan=Vec::new();
    for n in 0..singles+LIMIT {
        if n==singles&&!candidates.is_empty(){
            let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
            'setup:for &second in &role_changes{for &first in &setups{if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT/2{break 'setup;}}}}
            for &(first,second) in &role_pairs{if pair_plan.len()==LIMIT{break;}if seen.insert((first,second)){pair_plan.push((first,second));}}
            let mut k=0;
            while pair_plan.len()<LIMIT{pair_plan.push((k%candidates.len(),(k/candidates.len()+k+1)%candidates.len()));k+=1;}
        }
        let indices=if n<singles{vec![n]}else if candidates.is_empty(){break;}else{let (first,second)=pair_plan[n-singles];vec![first,second]};
        let mut engine=match fork(bound,fixture,probes.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
        out.sequences+=1;let mut steps=Vec::new();
        for i in indices{
            let (p,viewer,args)=&candidates[i];let before=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
            let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.args,args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history asset arguments: {why}",p.name));break;}};
            let mut prior=if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.target!="read"&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(RuleExpr::has_reference)){match fork(bound,&engine,Vec::new()){Ok(e)=>Some(e),Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(why);None}}}else{None};
            out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
            match engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)){
                Ok(result)=>{
                    let sent=matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..});
                    steps.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
                    let after=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);break;}};
                    if sent&&n<singles{
                        let signature=serde_json::to_string(&(&p.name,viewer,&candidates[i].2)).unwrap();
                        let new_media=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|inv|inv.target=="read"&&initial.get(&inv.table).is_none_or(Vec::is_empty)).any(|inv|{
                            let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
                            after.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten().any(|row|table.columns.iter().any(|(column,kind)|kind.contains_asset()&&row.field(column).is_ok_and(|v|*v!=Value::Null&&!matches!(v,Value::Array(xs) if xs.is_empty()))))
                        });
                        if new_media&&setup_seen.insert(signature.clone()){setups.push(i);}
                        let changed_role=bound.schema.tables.iter().any(|(table,_)|{
                            let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
                            !columns.is_empty()&&before.get(table).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|after.get(table).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|columns.iter().any(|c|old.field(c).ok()!=new.field(c).ok())))
                        });
                        if changed_role&&role_seen.insert(signature){role_changes.push(i);}
                    }
                    if sent{changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&steps,&mut prior,&mut engine,out);}else{out.refused+=1;}
                    if sent&&before!=after{observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&steps,out);column_reads::after_history(bound,&engine,&initial,&steps,out);}
                },Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history engine error {e}",p.name));break;}
            }
        }
    }
    out.unsupported.insert("history breadth/pairs bounded to 512; unvisited and longer traces remain uncovered".into());
}

//! Logical observer obligations, separate from the whole declaration verdict.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#101-separate-observer-obligations — pull group authority back to each contributor.
use super::*;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Obligation {
    pub path: String,
    pub status: Status,
    pub reason: String,
    pub repair: Option<String>,
}
impl Obligation {
    fn implication(path: String, admission: Result<RuleExpr, String>, policy: &RuleExpr) -> Self {
        let result = admission.and_then(|a| kernel::implies(&a, policy));
        let (status, reason, repair) = match result {
            Ok(true) => (Status::Proved, "every admitted logical contributor satisfies this policy".into(), None),
            Ok(false) => (Status::Unproved, "observer authority does not imply the source policy".into(),
                Some("retain the privacy fields as bare group keys and apply the source privacy policy to this observer".into())),
            Err(why) => (Status::Unproved, why, Some("use ordinary group keys and a read rule over those keys; keep opaque/temporal derivations private".into())),
        };
        Self { path, status, reason, repair }
    }
}

pub(super) fn obligations(bound: &Bound, inv: &Invariant) -> Vec<Obligation> {
    if inv.target != "read" { return Vec::new(); }
    let Some(policy) = &inv.policy else { return Vec::new(); };
    let table = &bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
    let mut out = vec![Obligation::implication(
        "server point/scan/planned view/count/sum/exists payload".into(),
        Ok(super::columns::admission(table,inv.column.as_deref())), policy)];
    for m in bound.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m| m.source == inv.table) {
        let admission = (|| {
            if m.row_key.is_some() || m.parent_key.is_some() || m.private.is_some()
                || !m.aliases.is_empty() || !m.computed.is_empty() || m.own_keys.is_some()
                || table.read_lifetime_sensitive()
            { return Err(format!("{} -> {}: captured/private/computed/temporal maintenance needs a contributor reduction", m.source, m.target)); }
            let target = bound.schema.table(&m.target).ok_or_else(|| format!("{}: maintained-column observer needs its owning table/field authority", m.target))?;
            if m.by.iter().any(|key|key.contains('.')||key.starts_with('$')||table.recorded.contains_key(key)
                || table.columns.get(key).is_none() || table.columns.get(key)!=target.columns.get(key)) {
                return Err(format!("{} -> {}: group keys are not bare identically typed source/target columns",m.source,m.target));
            }
            if target.through.is_some() || target.read_lifetime_sensitive() {
                return Err(format!("{}: derived observer has inherited/temporal authority", m.target));
            }
            // A target group field equals that field on every contributing
            // source row. Its id/value and all lost fields have no such law.
            pullback(&target.rules.read, &m.by)
        })();
        out.push(Obligation::implication(format!("maintained {:?}: {} -> {}", m.kind, m.source, m.target), admission, policy));
        for next in bound.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|n| n.source == m.target) {
            out.push(Obligation { path: format!("derived chain {} -> {} -> {}", inv.table, m.target, next.target),
                status: Status::Unproved, reason: "downstream contributor substitution is not implemented".into(),
                repair: Some("query the directly protected aggregate, or declare and verify privacy on each derived observer".into()) });
        }
    }
    out
}

fn pullback(rule: &RuleExpr, keys: &[String]) -> Result<RuleExpr, String> {
    fn term(t: &RuleTerm, keys: &[String]) -> Result<RuleTerm, String> {
        match t {
            RuleTerm::Viewer | RuleTerm::Literal(_) => Ok(t.clone()),
            RuleTerm::Field(f) if keys.contains(f) => Ok(t.clone()),
            _ => Err("derived rule reads a value/id/lost group field or a reference without a contributor identity".into()),
        }
    }
    Ok(match rule {
        RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Deny | RuleExpr::Public {..} => rule.clone(),
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b) => RuleExpr::Eq(term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
        RuleExpr::Ne(a,b) => RuleExpr::Ne(term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
        RuleExpr::Compare(op,a,b) => RuleExpr::Compare(*op,term(a,keys)?,term(b,keys)?),
        RuleExpr::And(xs) => RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|pullback(r,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
        RuleExpr::Or(xs) => RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|pullback(r,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?),
        RuleExpr::Not(x) => RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(pullback(x,keys)?)),
        RuleExpr::Exists {table,index,key,provenance,equalities} => RuleExpr::Exists {table:table.clone(),index:index.clone(),
            key:key.iter().map(|t|term(t,keys)).collect::<Result<_,_>>()?,provenance:*provenance,
            equalities:equalities.iter().map(|(n,t)|Ok((n.clone(),term(t,keys)?))).collect::<Result<_,String>>()?},
        RuleExpr::Created {..} => return Err("created probe has no derived read contributor reduction".into()),
    })
}
//! Conservative permission map; conditional predicates are never universal grants.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#5-exposure-report — include grant writers and implicit effects.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::ir;
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Grant {pub table:String,pub predicate:String,pub writers:Vec<String>}
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Exposure {pub table:String,pub columns:BTreeMap<String,String>,pub column_audiences:BTreeMap<String,String>,pub effects:BTreeMap<String,String>,pub operations:Vec<String>,pub paths:Vec<String>,pub grants:Vec<Grant>}
pub(super) fn mentions(json:&serde_json::Value,table:&str)->bool {
    match json {serde_json::Value::Object(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(k,v)| k=="table"&&v.as_str()==Some(table)||mentions(v,table)),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|x|mentions(x,table)),_=>false}
}
pub(super) fn authored_refusal(json:&serde_json::Value)->bool {
    match json {serde_json::Value::Object(fields)=>fields.get("Require").is_some()||fields.values().any(authored_refusal),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(authored_refusal),_=>false}
}
fn writers(bound:&Bound,name:&str)->Vec<String> {
    let mut out=Vec::new();let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];
    for effect in [Effect::Insert,Effect::Update] {out.push(format!("{effect} rule: {}",t.rules.for_effect(effect).quarry()));}
    for p in &bound.programs {for (table,kind) in ir::write_tables(&p.program) {if table==name {out.push(format!("{} ({kind}, {}; {})",p.program.name,p.program.kind.word(),if p.program.kind==ir::ProgramKind::Job {"system boundary"}else{"caller rules/field authority"}));}}}
    for (column,authority) in &t.written_by {out.push(format!("column {column}: owner .{} can patch under field authority",authority.owner));}
    for m in &bound.schema.maintains {if m.target==name||m.private.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|p.table==name){out.push(format!("system maintain {} from {}",m.target,m.source));}}
    if !t.on_delete.is_empty(){out.push(format!("referential actions: {:?}",t.on_delete));}
    if bound.programs.iter().any(|p|p.program.native)||!bound.schema.action_specs.is_empty()||!bound.schema.effects.is_empty(){out.push("native/worker/external paths: opaque trusted writers (not an absence claim)".into());}
    out.push("operator/system seed, import, restore and activation (trusted boundary)".into());out.sort();out.dedup();out
}
pub(super) fn exposures(bound:&Bound)->Vec<Exposure> {
    bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name,t)| {
        let columns=t.columns.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)).map(|(c,k)| {
            let authority=t.written_by.get(c).map(|a|format!("; written by .{}; {}",a.owner,if a.audience{"audience readable"}else{"private to owner"})).unwrap_or_default();
            (c.clone(),format!("{k}{authority}"))
        }).collect();
        let effects=[("read",t.rules.read.quarry()),("insert",t.rules.insert.quarry()),("update",t.update_rule_judged().quarry()),("delete",t.rules.delete.quarry())].into_iter().map(|(k,v)|(k.into(),v)).collect();
        let operations=bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|mentions(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),name)).map(|p|format!("{} ({})",p.program.name,p.program.kind.word())).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let mut paths=vec!["server point/list/filter/view/search/count/exists/live-refetch: ordinary ruled reads".into(),"mutation valued write images: authorized final reread; logical refusals separately unproved".into()];
        if t.is_expiring()||t.not_before.is_some(){paths.push(format!("live rows only: not before {:?}, expire {:?}; server clock gates delivery",t.not_before,t.expire));}
        if let Some(sync)=&t.sync {paths.push(format!("sync/device envelope: {}; device access uses delivered snapshot; leave {:?}",sync.line(),t.leave));}
        if t.columns.values().any(|k|k.contains_asset()){paths.push("asset read: owning-row read rule AND applicable owner-private column authority; upload placement: insert/update authority".into());}
        if let Some(through)=&t.through {paths.push(format!("read through {}.{} -> {} (capture/live reduction unproved)",name,through.reference,through.parent));}
        for m in &bound.schema.maintains {if m.source==*name||m.target==*name {paths.push(format!("derived {} -> {} (independent aggregate audience)",m.source,if m.target.starts_with("$column:"){ "maintained column" }else{&m.target}));}}
        if !t.on_delete.is_empty(){paths.push(format!("incoming referential actions bypass child rules: {:?}",t.on_delete));}
        let mut grants=Vec::new();let mut pending=Vec::new();let mut visited=BTreeSet::new();
        for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete] {for dep in kernel::dependencies(r){pending.push((dep,r.quarry()));}}
        while let Some((dep,predicate))=pending.pop(){if !visited.insert(dep.clone()){continue;}let Some(g)=bound.schema.table(&dep)else{continue};
            grants.push(Grant{table:dep.clone(),predicate,writers:writers(bound,&dep)});
            for r in [&g.rules.read,&g.rules.insert,&g.rules.update,&g.rules.delete]{for next in kernel::dependencies(r){pending.push((next,r.quarry()));}}
        }
        let column_audiences=t.columns.keys().filter(|c|!t.engine_field(c)).map(|c|(c.clone(),super::columns::admission(t,Some(c)).quarry())).collect();
        Exposure{table:name.clone(),columns,column_audiences,effects,operations,paths,grants}
    }).collect()
}
/// Role labels retain complete predicates, including relationships. Every
/// entry is conditional; a roster's existence is never assumed for all users.
pub(super) fn roles(exposures:&[Exposure])->BTreeMap<String,Vec<String>> {
    let mut out:BTreeMap<String,Vec<String>>=BTreeMap::new();
    for x in exposures {for (effect,predicate) in &x.effects {
        if predicate=="deny"{continue;}
        let label=if predicate=="allow"||predicate=="public"||predicate.starts_with("public "){"every signed-in user".into()}else{format!("caller satisfying {predicate}")};
        out.entry(label).or_default().push(format!("{} {effect}: {predicate}; operations: {}",x.table,x.operations.join(", ")));
    }}out
}
//! Effective column payload audience; unresolved observer edges stay explicit.
use super::*;
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#121-column-read-intent — a private owner gate narrows only the column payload.
pub(super) fn admission(table:&snapback4_core::schema::Table,column:Option<&str>)->RuleExpr{
    let mut rules=vec![super::temporal::admission(table)];
    if let Some(owner)=column.and_then(|c|table.written_by.get(c)).filter(|o|!o.audience){
        rules.push(RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(owner.owner.clone()),RuleTerm::Viewer));
    }
    RuleExpr::And(rules)
}
//! Closed enum census: a new IR statement/expression/builtin forces review.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#3-sound-proof-boundary — opaque context changes are blockers.
use snapback4_core::ir::{Expr,Stmt,Builtin};
pub(super) fn body(xs:&[Stmt])->bool{xs.iter().all(|s|match s{
    Stmt::Const{value,..}|Stmt::Let{value,..}|Stmt::Assign{value,..}|Stmt::Push{value,..}|Stmt::Return(value)=>expr(value),
    Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body}=>expr(condition)&&body(then_body)&&body(else_body),
    Stmt::For{..}=>false,
    Stmt::Require{condition,..}|Stmt::Check{condition,..}=>expr(condition),
    Stmt::Insert{row,..}|Stmt::InsertIfAbsent{row,..}|Stmt::Upsert{row,..}=>expr(row),
    Stmt::Update{key,patch,..}=>expr(key)&&expr(patch),
    Stmt::WriteTarget{patch,condition,..}=>expr(patch)&&condition.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|body(&c.setup)&&expr(&c.value)),
    Stmt::Delete{key,..}=>expr(key),Stmt::DeleteTarget{..}|Stmt::Break|Stmt::Continue=>true,
    Stmt::Emit{..}=>false,
})}
fn expr(e:&Expr)->bool{match e{
    Expr::Literal(_)|Expr::Variable(_)|Expr::Input(_)=>true,
    Expr::Field{base,..}|Expr::Unary{value:base,..}=>expr(base),
    Expr::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().all(expr),Expr::Object(xs)=>xs.values().all(expr),
    Expr::Shape{base,fields}=>expr(base)&&fields.values().all(expr),
    Expr::Binary{left,right,..}=>expr(left)&&expr(right),
    Expr::Block{body:xs,value}=>body(xs)&&expr(value),
    Expr::Get(r)|Expr::Exists(r)=>r.key.iter().all(expr),
    Expr::Scan(_)|Expr::Merge(_)=>false,
    Expr::Builtin{builtin,args}=>pure(builtin)&&args.iter().all(expr),
}}
fn pure(b:&Builtin)->bool{match b{
    Builtin::JobAs|Builtin::JobQuery{..}=>false,
    Builtin::ProjectFields{..}|Builtin::ReceiptSettled{..}|Builtin::OptionalField{..}|Builtin::Calendar{..}|Builtin::Civil(_)|Builtin::Len|Builtin::Slice|Builtin::Concat|Builtin::Lower|Builtin::Trim|Builtin::Sha256|Builtin::Join|Builtin::Group{..}|Builtin::First|Builtin::Last|Builtin::At|Builtin::Min|Builtin::Max|Builtin::Extremum{..}|Builtin::ExtremumOf{..}|Builtin::Divmod{..}|Builtin::Divide{..}|Builtin::Sort{..}|Builtin::MentionTokens|Builtin::SearchPresent|Builtin::RequireUncapped|Builtin::DeterministicShare|Builtin::Count|Builtin::Sum|Builtin::CountCapped|Builtin::Connected=>true,
}}

        let paths=exposures.iter().find(|x|x.table==inv.table).map(|x|x.paths.clone()).unwrap_or_default();

        exclusions:vec!["system-principal trust boundary".into(),"physical timing, examined counts and resource/meter side channels".into(),"offline access may use a snapshot predating server revocation".into()],invariants:verdicts,exposures,roles,notes,uncovered,campaign_coverage:BTreeSet::new()}
}
fn blockers(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Vec<String> {
    let schema=&bound.schema;let t=&schema.tables[&inv.table];let mut out=BTreeSet::new();
    if inv.target=="read" && inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|policy|kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,policy)!=Ok(true)) {
        out.insert("wire sequencing/activity metadata has no logical-existence reduction; direct row-rule proof does not establish whole-protocol noninterference".into());
    }
    if inv.target=="read" {
        if let Some(policy)=&inv.policy {
            let grants=policy.disjuncts().into_iter().filter(|r|!r.any_term(&|term|!matches!(term,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_)))).cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>();
            let global=RuleExpr::Or(grants);
            if !matches!(t.rules.insert,RuleExpr::Deny) && kernel::implies(&t.rules.insert,&global)!=Ok(true) {
                out.insert("insert/unique/upsert collision outcomes can reveal an existing hidden row; old-row read authorization is not proved by new-image insert authority".into());
            }
            if kernel::implies(&t.update_rule_judged(),&policy.old_and_next())!=Ok(true) && !matches!(t.rules.update,RuleExpr::Deny) {
                out.insert("blind update/upsert outcomes can reveal hidden-target existence; read correspondence is unproved".into());
            }
        }
        if !t.written_by.is_empty(){out.insert("owner-field write outcomes can observe targets outside the row read audience".into());}
    }
    if inv.target=="read" && t.columns.iter().any(|(c,k)| matches!(k.required(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Ref{..}) && t.on_delete.get(c).copied().unwrap_or(snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Restrict).acts()) {
        out.insert("parent deletion/referential refusal can observe this child (including default restrict and transitive closures)".into());
    }
    if inv.target=="read" && t.sync.is_some(){out.insert("device read uses a delivered snapshot; current server role at disconnected access is unproved".into());}
    if t.through.is_some(){out.insert("inherited/captured or live-parent authority requires a separate reduction (RULES5 owns live-parent semantics)".into());}
    if !schema.receipts.is_empty() || !schema.timelines.is_empty(){out.insert("receipt/timeline engine-owned paths require a separate reduction; no affected-table inference is assumed".into());}
    if t.is_expiring() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"delete"|"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("expiry/commit-end retirement can change rows outside ordinary delete rules".into());}
    if t.ephemeral.is_some() || !t.sequences.is_empty(){out.insert("ephemeral/sequence path is outside the current proof reduction".into());}
    if !t.written_by.is_empty() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("owned-field update bypass: table rule alone does not establish field-write authority".into());}
    for (name,other) in &schema.tables {
        if matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") && other.on_delete.values().any(|a|matches!(a,snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::Cascade|snapback4_core::schema::references::OnDelete::SetNull)) && name==&inv.table {
            out.insert("incoming cascade/set-null changes bypass this table's ordinary rules".into());
        }
        if inv.target=="read" {
            for rule in [&other.rules.read,&other.rules.insert,&other.rules.update,&other.rules.delete] {
                if kernel::dependencies(rule).contains(&inv.table) {out.insert(format!("raw rule probe/reference from {name} can observe {} independently of its read rule",inv.table));}
            }
        }
    }
    if schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.target==inv.table) && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"write"|"always") {out.insert("aggregate/view target rows are written by engine maintenance independently of their direct table rules".into());}
    if !t.maintained.is_empty() && matches!(inv.target.as_str(),"write"|"update"|"always"){out.insert("engine-maintained columns bypass ordinary application write rules".into());}
    if bound.programs.iter().any(|p|matches!(p.program.kind,snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job|snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Action)|p.program.native)
        || !schema.effects.is_empty() || !schema.action_specs.is_empty() {
        out.insert("system/job/action/native/external path requires a reviewed reduction; no affected-table inference is assumed".into());
    }
    if inv.target=="read" {
        for p in &bound.programs {
            let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();
            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && !census::body(&p.program.body) {out.insert(format!("{}: scan/merge/loop or opaque IR path needs a reviewed read-disclosure reduction",p.program.name));}
            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(target,_)|target!=&inv.table) {
                out.insert(format!("{} may copy protected data into another table; cross-table disclosure reduction is unproved",p.program.name));
            }
            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && audit::authored_refusal(&json) {out.insert(format!("{}: authored refusal observations are not reduced to this read policy",p.program.name));}
        }
    }
    out.into_iter().collect()
}

pub fn add_source_notes(report:&mut Report,sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound){
    report.notes.extend(lints::source_notes(sources,bound));
    let mut sites=BTreeMap::new();
    for (file,source) in sources{let (doc,_)=crate::parse_document(file,source);for t in doc.tables{
        sites.insert(t.name.clone(),t.site.clone());for c in t.columns{sites.insert(format!("{}.{}",t.name,c.name),c.site);}
        for r in t.rules{sites.insert(format!("{}.{}",t.name,r.effect.word()),r.site);}
    }}
    for note in &mut report.notes{if note.site.line==0 {
        let key=note.message.split([' ',':']).next().unwrap_or("");
        note.site=sites.get(key).or_else(||sites.get(key.split('.').next().unwrap_or(""))).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Site::new(sources.first().map(|s|s.0.as_str()).unwrap_or("schema.q"),1,1));
    }}
}

//! Saved-source fixture selection, business cases and the query sweep.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#9-wave-13-saved-version-proofs-day3-2026-10-16 — contemporary fixtures belong to the selected saved source.
use super::*;

const CASE_SUMMARY: &str = "SNAPBACK4_TEST_CASES";

fn case_count(path: &std::path::Path) -> Result<usize, String> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|error| format!("cannot read {}: {error}", path.display()))?;
    Ok(text.lines().filter(|line| {
        let line = line.trim();
        !line.is_empty() && !line.starts_with("//")
    }).count())
}

/// `test`: run every argument-free query as each persona against the store
/// (optionally loaded from a fixture) and print the meters beside the
/// ceilings, so a builder reads numbers instead of being refused on a bound.
pub fn test(options: &Options) -> i32 {
    let project = match proof_source::project(options, "source") { Ok(project) => project, Err(e) => { eprintln!("test: {e}"); return 2; } };
    let sources = match proof_source::sources(&project, options, "source") { Ok(sources) => sources, Err(e) => { eprintln!("test: {e}"); return 2; } };
    let bound = compiled_or_exit(proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "source"), options.json);
    let mut defaults = options.clone();
    let mut skipped_business_cases = 0;
    if !defaults.flags.contains_key("fixture") {
        let saved = options.flags.get("source").map(std::path::Path::new).map(|path| {
            if path.is_dir() { path.join("fixtures") }
            else if path.file_name().is_some_and(|name| name == "schema.q") { path.parent().unwrap().join("fixtures") }
            else { path.with_file_name(format!("{}-fixtures", path.file_stem().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy())) }
        });
        if let Some(fixture) = saved.filter(|path| path.is_dir()) {
            eprintln!("test: using {} (saved version's contemporary fixtures; override with --fixture <dir>)", fixture.display());
            defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(), fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        } else if project.root.join("fixtures").is_dir() {
            let fixture = project.root.join("fixtures");
            if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
                defaults.flags.insert("saved-fixture-fallback".into(), String::new());
                eprintln!("test: no contemporary fixtures for saved source; validating only declared-table files in {}; pass --fixture <dir> for a complete contemporary case suite", fixture.display());
                let cases = fixture.join(test_cases::FILE);
                if cases.is_file() {
                    skipped_business_cases = match case_count(&cases) { Ok(count) => count, Err(error) => { eprintln!("test: {error}"); return 1; } };
                    eprintln!("test: skipped {} — working cases may target another release; pass --fixture <dir> to run explicitly", cases.display());
                }
            } else { eprintln!("test: using {} (default fixture directory; override with --fixture <dir>)", fixture.display()); }
            defaults.flags.insert("fixture".into(), fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());
        } else if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
            eprintln!("test: no saved or working fixtures; query sweep only; pass --fixture <dir> for business cases");
        }
    }
    let options = &defaults;
    // test applies dev's cast: a persona dev would refuse is not run as an ordinary viewer.
    // Residency measures any principal in the data (scale fixtures name thousands), so it notes instead.
    if let Some(why) = options.flags.get("as").and_then(|names| names.split(',').find_map(|name| test_cases::undeclared_persona(name, &bound.personas))) {
        if !options.flags.contains_key("residency") {
            eprintln!("test --as: {why}");
            return 1;
        }
        eprintln!("test --residency --as: measuring an undeclared principal; dev would refuse it as a persona");
    }
    let _owner = if options.flags.contains_key("fixture") || options.flags.contains_key("source") { None } else {
        match crate::store_owner::command(&project.store_path()) {
            Ok(owner) => Some(owner),
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
        }
    };
    let fixture_now = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    let opened = match &_owner {
        Some(owner) => owner.open_engine(&bound, std::time::Duration::from_secs(5), fixture_now).map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
        None => test_fixture::engine_at(&project, &bound, options, fixture_now),
    };
    let mut engine = match opened {
        Ok(engine) => engine,
        Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
    };
    let personas = residency::personas(&bound, options);
    let sweep_personas = if options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback") { Default::default() } else {
        match test_sweep::load(options.flags.get("fixture"), &bound) {
            Ok(matrix) => matrix,
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
        }
    };
    if let Some(persona) = options.flags.get("as") && !sweep_personas.is_empty() {
        println!("test --as {persona} overrides test.sweep.json because --as requests one deliberate global probe; for: {}", sweep_personas.keys().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "));
    }
    if options.flags.contains_key("residency") {
        let verbose = options.flags.contains_key("verbose");
        if let Err(error) = residency::measure(&mut engine, &bound, &personas, verbose) { eprintln!("residency measurement failed: {error}"); return 1; }
        if !verbose {
            if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
                println!("{CASE_SUMMARY} {{\"run\":0,\"skipped\":{skipped_business_cases}}}");
            }
            if !options.flags.contains_key("fixture-only") {
                eprintln!("test --residency executed no business cases; use --fixture-only for acquisition measurement, or omit --residency to run the cases");
                return 1;
            }
            return 0;
        }
    }
    let mut security_campaign = snapback4_lang::security::challenges::Campaign::default();
    if options.flags.contains_key("fixture"){
        match test_fixture::engine_at(&project,&bound,options,snapback4_lang::security::challenges::NOW){
            Ok(security_fixture)=>snapback4_lang::security::challenges::operations(&bound,&security_fixture,&mut security_campaign),
            Err(why)=>{security_campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("trusted fixture campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
        }
    }else{
        security_campaign.unsupported.insert("no trusted fixture files for the fixed campaign clock; development store is not replayed".into());
        if let Ok(store)=snapback4_core::Store::memory(){
            match snapback4_core::Engine::new_at(store,bound.schema.clone(),bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone()),snapback4_lang::security::challenges::NOW){
                Ok(isolated)=>snapback4_lang::security::challenges::operations(&bound,&isolated,&mut security_campaign),
                Err(why)=>{security_campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("schema matrix unavailable: {why}"));}
            }
        }
    }
    let security_campaign=snapback4_lang::security::challenges::run_selected_after(&bound,None,security_campaign);
    let mut security_campaign=security_campaign;
    let security_http_ok=security::unauthenticated(&bound,&sources);
    if security_http_ok{security_campaign.unsupported.retain(|s|!s.starts_with("unauthenticated HTTP attempts require"));}
    println!("security histories: {} ordered short operation sequences replayed",security_campaign.sequences);
    for why in &security_campaign.boundary_failures {println!("security boundary failure: {why}");}
    println!("security operation matrix: {} operations exercised; unknown authorization intent is not inferred from success",security_campaign.operations.len());
    let reachable=security_campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|c.reachability==snapback4_lang::security::challenges::Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace).count();
    println!("security tests: {} attempted, {} refused, {reachable} reachable violations; {} seed/synthetic observations (non-failing); finite campaign, not proof",security_campaign.attempted,security_campaign.refused,security_campaign.violations.len()-reachable);
    for c in security_campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|options.flags.contains_key("all")||c.reachability==snapback4_lang::security::challenges::Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace) {println!("security violation: {}",serde_json::to_string(c).unwrap());}
    for reason in &security_campaign.unsupported {println!("security not covered: {reason}");}
    let (mut passed, mut failed_cases) = (0, 0);
    if let Some(fixture) = options.flags.get("fixture").filter(|_| !options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback")) {
        match test_cases::run_verbose(&mut engine, &bound, fixture, &sources, fixture_now, options.flags.contains_key("verbose")) {
            Ok((p, f)) => { passed = p; failed_cases = f; }
            Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); return 1; }
        }
    }
    let ceilings = engine.ceilings();
    let mut failures = 0;
    println!("test: {:<28} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>10} {:>10}", "operation (persona)", "examined", "entries", "probes", "steps", "heap B", "output B");
    println!("      {:<28} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>10} {:>10}", "ceilings", ceilings.examined, ceilings.index_entries, ceilings.rule_probes, ceilings.steps, ceilings.heap_bytes, ceilings.output_bytes);
    println!("legend: examined=rows visited; entries=index visits; probes=rule lookups; steps=interpreter work; heap B=charged heap; output B=encoded answer.");
    println!("verdicts: complete=whole answer; capped=partial; refused=expected-case or program-require refusal; denied=unexpected refusal; not run=no coverage; case OK=expectation passed.");
    let mut skipped = Vec::new();
    let mut held_not_run = 0;
    let mut near = Vec::new();
    let (mut complete, mut capped, mut refused_derived, mut expected) = (0usize, 0usize, 0usize, 0usize);
    let explicit = options.flags.get("fixture").filter(|_| !options.flags.contains_key("saved-fixture-fallback")).map(|fixture| test_cases::explicit(fixture, &bound, fixture_now)).unwrap_or_default();
    for program in &bound.programs {
        if program.program.kind != ProgramKind::Query {
            continue;
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.1 — a server fixture is not the device's held partition.
        if bound.schema.operations.get(&program.program.name).is_some_and(|fact| fact.held) {
            println!("      not run: {} is held (device only)", program.program.name);
            held_not_run += 1;
            continue;
        }
        let selected = if options.flags.contains_key("as") { &personas }
            else { sweep_personas.get(&program.program.name).unwrap_or(&personas) };
        for persona in selected {
            let principal = if persona.contains(':') { persona.clone() } else { persona_principal(persona) };
            let name = &program.program.name;
            let cases: Vec<&test_cases::Explicit> = explicit.iter().filter(|case| case.op == *name && case.principal == principal).collect();
            // Arguments: the persona's own case for this query when it has
            // one (the builder's choice, run as written); otherwise derived
            // from the data: a reference is a row of its table, preferring
            // one whose answer is not empty (the first row is often another
            // persona's, hidden), a principal is the persona, a text is the
            // shortest that fits. What cannot be derived is named, never
            // skipped silently.
            // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.2 — wave 7: derived arguments tested nothing when the first row was hidden from the persona.
            let mut candidates: Vec<(BTreeMap<String, snapback4_core::Value>, Option<&str>)> = Vec::new();
            if let Some(case) = cases.iter().find(|case| case.args.is_some()) {
                let decoded: Option<BTreeMap<_, _>> = case.args.as_ref().and_then(Json::as_object).and_then(|fields| fields.iter().map(|(arg, value)| {
                    let kind = program.program.args.get(arg)?;
                    wire::from_json(kind, value).ok().map(|value| (arg.clone(), value))
                }).collect());
                if let Some(args) = decoded { candidates.push((args, Some(case.at.as_str()))); }
            }
            let mut missing = Vec::new();
            if candidates.is_empty() {
                let rows = program.program.args.values().filter_map(|kind| match kind { ir::ArgType::Ref { table } => engine.store().rows(table).ok().map(|rows| rows.len()), _ => None }).max().unwrap_or(1).clamp(1, DERIVED_ROWS);
                for at in 0..rows {
                    let mut args = BTreeMap::new();
                    for (arg, kind) in &program.program.args {
                        match sample_arg(&engine, kind, &principal, at) {
                            Some(value) => { args.insert(arg.clone(), value); }
                            None if at == 0 => missing.push(format!("{arg}: {}", arg_word(kind))),
                            None => {}
                        }
                    }
                    if !missing.is_empty() { break; }
                    candidates.push((args, None));
                }
            }
            if !missing.is_empty() {
                skipped.push(format!("{name} ({persona}): needs {}", missing.join(", ")));
                continue;
            }
            // The first answer with rows wins; else the first answer; else the first refusal.
            let mut chosen: Option<(usize, BTreeMap<String, snapback4_core::Value>, Option<&str>, Result<snapback4_core::QueryOutcome, snapback4_core::Refusal>, f64)> = None;
            let tried = candidates.len();
            for (args, source) in candidates {
                let started = Instant::now();
                let result = engine.query(name, Context::new(principal.clone(), args.clone()).at(fixture_now));
                let ms = started.elapsed().as_secs_f64() * 1000.0;
                let rank = match &result { Ok(outcome) if !empty_answer(&outcome.data) => 0, Ok(_) => 1, Err(_) => 2 };
                if chosen.as_ref().is_none_or(|(best, ..)| rank < *best) { chosen = Some((rank, args, source, result, ms)); }
                if rank == 0 || source.is_some() { break; }
            }
            let Some((rank, args, source, result, ms)) = chosen else { continue };
            let shown = if args.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!(" {}", serde_json::to_string(&args.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.clone(), wire::to_json(v))).collect::<serde_json::Map<_, _>>()).unwrap_or_default()) };
            let arguments = shown.clone();
            let shown = match source { Some(at) => format!("{shown} (from {at})"), None => shown };
            let derived = !args.is_empty() && source.is_none();
            let label = format!("{name} ({persona})");
            match result {
                Ok(outcome) => {
                    let m = outcome.metered;
                    let state = if outcome.complete { "complete" } else { "capped" };
                    if outcome.complete { complete += 1 } else { capped += 1 }
                    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.22.1 — report approach to a ceiling on the fixture, separately from actual cuts.
                    if outcome.capped_at.is_none() && outcome.word_cut_at.is_none() {
                        if let Some(cost) = crate::serve::read_cost::near_ceiling(m, ceilings) { near.push(format!("{label} {cost}")); }
                    }
                    let empty = if derived && rank == 1 && tried > 1 { format!(" — empty for each of {tried} derived rows: give it yours in {}", test_cases::FILE) } else { String::new() };
                    println!("      {label:<28} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>8} {:>10} {:>10}  {state} in {ms:.1} ms{shown}{empty}", m.examined, m.index_entries, m.rule_probes, m.steps, m.heap_bytes, m.output_bytes);
                }
                // A refusal the persona's own case expects is what the builder
                // asked for: say so instead of suggesting a case that exists.
                Err(refusal) if cases.iter().any(|case| case.expect.as_deref() == Some(refusal.code.as_str())) => {
                    expected += 1;
                    let at = cases.iter().find(|case| case.expect.as_deref() == Some(refusal.code.as_str())).map(|case| case.at.as_str()).unwrap_or_default();
                    println!("      {label:<28} refused {} as expected ({at}){}", refusal.code, if source == Some(at) { arguments } else { shown });
                }
                Err(refusal) if refusal.family == "rule" => {
                    // A program's own `require` refusing this persona is
                    // information, not a failure: the persona is not in that
                    // group. The caller chose nothing on this pass, whether
                    // the arguments were derived or there are none (wave 5
                    // lane 059: an argument-free gate counted as denied).
                    // @ref LLP 3000.036#15.2 — the derived pass never fails on the program's own require.
                    refused_derived += 1;
                    if let Some(at) = source {
                        println!("      {label:<28} refused {} on your arguments{shown} — the case at {at} runs below", refusal.code);
                    } else if derived {
                        let rows = if tried > 1 { format!(" (each of {tried} rows)") } else { String::new() };
                        println!("      {label:<28} refused {} on derived arguments{shown}{rows} — give it yours in {}: {{\"op\":\"{name}\",\"as\":\"{persona}\",\"args\":{{..}}}}", refusal.code, test_cases::FILE);
                    } else {
                        println!("      {label:<28} refused {} by its own require (not a failure: {persona} is not admitted) — pin it in {}: {{\"op\":\"{name}\",\"as\":\"{persona}\",\"expect\":\"{}\"}}", refusal.code, test_cases::FILE, refusal.code);
                    }
                }
                Err(refusal) => {
                    failures += 1;
                    println!("      {label:<28} denied {}: {}{shown}", refusal.code, refusal.message);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    for line in near.iter().take(10) { println!("near ceiling: {line}"); }
    if near.len() > 10 { println!("near ceiling: {} more query/persona reads passed half a ceiling without a cut", near.len() - 10); }
    for line in &skipped {
        println!("      not run: {line} — add a row of that table to the fixture, or a case in {}", test_cases::FILE);
    }
    // One line a script or an agent can read: derived refusals and unrun
    // queries are not failures, but they are not coverage either.
    let no_cases = passed + failed_cases == 0 && !options.flags.contains_key("fixture-only");
    let security_failed = security_campaign.violations.iter().any(|c|c.reachability==snapback4_lang::security::challenges::Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace) || !security_campaign.boundary_failures.is_empty() || !security_http_ok;
    let verdict = if failures > 0 || failed_cases > 0 || no_cases || security_failed { "FAIL" } else if !skipped.is_empty() || held_not_run > 0 { "incomplete" } else { "pass" };
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#9 — derived probe refusals are expected exploration, not failed cases.
    println!("test: {complete} complete, {capped} capped, {expected} refused as your cases expect, {refused_derived} probe refusals on derived arguments (expected), {} not run, {failures} denied{} — {verdict}",
        skipped.len() + held_not_run, if passed + failed_cases > 0 { format!("; cases: {passed} passed, {failed_cases} failed") } else if options.flags.contains_key("fixture") { format!("; no {} (see snapback4 guide testing)", test_cases::FILE) } else { String::new() });
    // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#11-wave-14-truthful-starter-custody-day4 — query-sweep reads are not application business cases.
    if options.flags.contains_key("source") {
        println!("{CASE_SUMMARY} {{\"run\":{},\"skipped\":{skipped_business_cases}}}", passed + failed_cases);
    }
    if no_cases {
        eprintln!("test: no business cases executed ({skipped_business_cases} skipped); add test.cases.jsonl, or use --fixture-only when fixture loading/query probes are the intended check");
    }
    if failures > 0 || failed_cases > 0 || no_cases || security_failed { 1 } else { 0 }
}
