//! Concrete, isolated challenges; finite campaigns never establish proof.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#4-counterexamples-and-generated-tests — only replayed admission is a refutation.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::{Engine,Store,Context,Value,RuntimeError};
use snapback4_core::schema::{ColumnType as C,Constraint};
use snapback4_core::engine::rules::{self,Probe};
use snapback4_core::ir::{self,Expr,Stmt,Program,ProgramKind,Read,KeyProvenance};
pub const NOW:i64=1_800_000_000_000;
const VIEWER:&str="dev:security-a";
const OTHER:&str="dev:security-b";
// Same verified 1x1 PNG header used by the existing packaged asset witnesses.
const PNG:[u8;24]=[137,80,78,71,13,10,26,10,0,0,0,13,73,72,68,82,0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1];
mod paired;
mod timed;
mod histories;
mod assets;
mod column_reads;
mod stability;
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,PartialEq,Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all="kebab-case")]
pub enum Reachability { AuthoredFixtureTrace, TrustedSeedOnly, SyntheticOperation }
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct Counterexample {pub reachability:Reachability,pub invariant_site:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub viewer:String,pub operation:String,pub effect:String,pub row:Value,pub old:Option<Value>,pub supporting_rows:BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,pub rule_path:String,pub scope:String, pub observations:Option<paired::Observations>,pub history:Vec<histories::Step>}
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Default,Serialize)]
pub struct Campaign {pub paired_observer_calls:usize,pub history_observer_calls:usize,pub column_observer_calls:usize,pub operations:BTreeMap<String,usize>,pub sequences:usize,pub attempted:usize,pub admitted:usize,pub refused:usize,pub violations:Vec<Counterexample>,pub boundary_failures:Vec<String>,pub unsupported:BTreeSet<String>}
fn id(table:&str)->Value {Value::Id(format!("security-{table}"))}
fn sample(kind:&C,variant:usize)->Option<Value>{Some(match kind{
    C::Optional(inner)=>if variant&4!=0{sample(inner,variant).unwrap_or(Value::Null)}else{Value::Null},C::Id|C::Principal=>Value::Id(if variant&1==0{VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into()),
    C::Ref{table}=>id(table),C::Text{max}=>Value::String("x".repeat((*max).min(20) as usize)),
    C::Enum{variants}=>Value::String(variants.get(variant%variants.len())?.clone()),
    C::Int|C::Decimal{..}|C::Money{..}=>Value::Int(1),C::IntRange{min,max}=>Value::Int(1i64.clamp(*min,*max)),
    C::Bool=>Value::Bool((variant>>2)&1==0),C::Time=>Value::Int(NOW-1000),C::Json{..}=>Value::Null,C::Bytes{..}=>Value::Bytes(Vec::new()),
    C::List{..}=>Value::Array(Vec::new()),C::ListRange{of,min,..}=>Value::Array((0..*min).map(|_|sample(of,variant)).collect::<Option<_>>()?),
    C::Image{..}=>Value::String("$security:image".into()),C::File{..}=>Value::String("$security:file".into()),C::Video{..}=>return None,
})}
fn needed(schema:&Schema,name:&str,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){
    if !out.insert(name.into()){return;}let Some(t)=schema.table(name)else{return};
    for m in schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.target==name){needed(schema,&m.source,out);}
    for k in t.columns.values(){if let C::Ref{table}=k.required(){needed(schema,table,out);}}
    for r in [&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete]{for dep in kernel::dependencies(r){needed(schema,&dep,out);}}
}
fn snapshot(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    snapshot_mode(bound,inv,variant,false)
}
fn snapshot_mode(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,variant:usize,timed:bool)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{
    let mut names=BTreeSet::new();needed(&bound.schema,&inv.table,&mut names);
    if let Some(policy)=&inv.policy{for dep in kernel::dependencies(policy){needed(&bound.schema,&dep,&mut names);}}
    if let Some(mut syntax)=inv.syntax.clone(){super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut syntax,&mut |e| {
        if let ast::Expr::Read(r)|ast::Expr::Exists{read:r,..}=e {needed(&bound.schema,&r.table,&mut names);}
    });}
    if let Some(op)=&inv.operation && let Some(p)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==op){for kind in p.program.args.values(){if let ir::ArgType::Ref{table}=kind{needed(&bound.schema,table,&mut names);}}}
    let mut out=BTreeMap::new();
    let mut enum_case=variant;
    for name in names {
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&name];
        if bound.schema.maintain_for(&name).is_some(){continue;}
        let mut fields=BTreeMap::new();fields.insert("id".into(),id(&name));
        let mut principal_index=0usize;
        for (n,k) in &t.columns{
            if n=="id"||t.engine_field(n)||t.maintained.contains_key(n){continue;}
            let sample_variant=if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){let n=enum_case % variants.len();enum_case/=variants.len();n}else if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal){let n=((variant>>((if name==inv.table{0}else{3})+principal_index.min(5)))&1)|(variant&4);principal_index+=1;n}else{variant};
            let value=sample(k,sample_variant)
                .ok_or_else(||format!("{name}.{n}: asset ledger/required sample is not available"))?;
            fields.insert(n.clone(),value);
        }
        if timed || t.read_lifetime_sensitive() {for (n,k) in &t.columns {if matches!(k.required(),C::Time)&&(!k.is_optional()||variant&4!=0) {
            fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Int(NOW+if t.expire.as_ref()==Some(n){2000}else if timed{1000}else{-1000}));
        }}}
        for constraint in &t.constraints{if let Constraint::Text{field,min,..}=constraint
            && matches!(fields.get(field),Some(Value::String(_))) {fields.insert(field.clone(),Value::String("x".repeat((*min).max(1) as usize)));}}
        // Named primary keys used by role probes must select the challenged
        // viewer; changing the enum represents a current-role change while
        // derived assignment/membership rows remain present.
        if name!=inv.table {for (i,(n,k)) in t.columns.iter().filter(|(_,k)|matches!(k,C::Principal)).enumerate(){fields.insert(n.clone(),Value::Id(if variant & (1 << (i+3)) == 0 {VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into())); let _=k;}}
        out.insert(name,vec![Value::Row(fields.into())]);
    }
    Ok(out)
}
struct Snapshot<'a>{schema:&'a Schema,rows:&'a BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>}
impl Probe for Snapshot<'_>{
    fn exists(&mut self,table:&str,index:&str,key:&[Value],equalities:&[(String,Value)])->Result<bool,RuntimeError>{
        let t=&self.schema.tables[table];let Some(idx)=t.index(index)else{return Ok(false)};
        if idx.components.len()!=key.len(){return Ok(false);}
        if idx.unique&&idx.components.iter().any(|c|c.column().is_some_and(|n|t.columns.get(n).is_some_and(C::is_optional)))&&key.contains(&Value::Null){return Ok(false);}
        Ok(self.rows.get(table).into_iter().flatten().filter(|r|live(t,r)).any(|r|idx.components.iter().zip(key).all(|(c,v)|c.column().is_some_and(|n|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v)))&&equalities.iter().all(|(n,v)|r.field(n).ok()==Some(v))))
    }
    fn created(&mut self,_:&str,_:&str,_:&[Value])->bool{false}

}
fn live(t:&snapback4_core::schema::Table,row:&Value)->bool{
    !t.expired(row,NOW)&&t.not_before.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|match row.field(c){Ok(Value::Int(n))=>*n<=NOW,Ok(Value::Null)=>true,_=>false})
}
fn policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value)->Result<bool,String>{

    let Some(p)=inv.policy.as_ref() else{return inv.syntax.as_ref().and_then(|e|super::concrete::evaluate(e,row,rows,&bound.schema,viewer)).ok_or_else(||"concrete policy expression is unknown/unsupported (including clock and typed literal coercions)".into());};

    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}
    if p.has_reference(){return Err("reference intent requires authorized engine policy replay".into());}
    rules::evaluate(p,viewer,old,&image,false,&mut Snapshot{schema:&bound.schema,rows}).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
fn policy_in(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value,engine:&mut Engine)->Result<bool,String>{
    let Some(p)=&inv.policy else{return policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row);};
    if !p.has_reference(){return policy(bound,inv,rows,viewer,old,row);}
    let mut image=row.clone();if super::temporal::has_clock(p)&&let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image{fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(NOW));}
    match engine.fixture_policy(p,viewer,old,&image,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?{
        rules::Verdict::Readable=>Ok(true),rules::Verdict::Denied=>Ok(false),rules::Verdict::Unknown=>Err("policy reference/coverage judgment is unknown; never a refutation".into()),
    }
}
fn program(name:&str,kind:ProgramKind,body:Vec<Stmt>)->Program{Program{name:name.into(),kind,args:BTreeMap::new(),body,read_time_granularity_ms:None,returned_shapes:BTreeMap::new(),native:false}}
fn engine(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probe:Program)->Result<Engine,String>{
    engine_with(bound,rows,vec![probe])
}
fn engine_with(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probes:Vec<Program>)->Result<Engine,String>{
    engine_on_store(bound,rows,probes,Store::memory().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)
}
fn engine_on_store(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,probes:Vec<Program>,store:Store)->Result<Engine,String>{
    let mut programs=bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>();programs.extend(probes);
    let mut engine=Engine::new_at(store,bound.schema.clone(),programs,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    fn hydrate(engine:&mut Engine,kind:&C,value:&Value)->Result<Value,String>{
        match (kind,value) {
            (C::Optional(inner),v) if *v!=Value::Null=>hydrate(engine,inner,v),
            (C::List{of,..}|C::ListRange{of,..},Value::Array(xs))=>xs.iter().map(|v|hydrate(engine,of,v)).collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>().map(Value::Array),
            (C::Image{..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:image"=>engine.store_asset(VIEWER,&PNG,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|format!("verified image fixture: {e}")),
            (C::File{types,..},Value::String(marker)) if marker=="$security:file"=>assets::file(engine,VIEWER,types),
            _=>Ok(value.clone()),
        }
    }
    let mut seeded=rows.iter().filter(|(name,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(name).is_none()).map(|(name,rs)|{
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];
        let clean=rs.iter().map(|r|if let Value::Row(fields)=r {Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect())}else{r.clone()}).collect();
        (name.clone(),clean)
    }).collect::<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>>();
    for (table,values) in &mut seeded {for row in values {for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns {
        if let Ok(v)=row.field(column) {
            let value=hydrate(&mut engine,kind,v)?;
            if let Value::Row(fields)=row {fields.insert(column.clone(),value);}
        }
    }}}
    engine.seed_at(seeded.iter().flat_map(|(t,rs)|rs.iter().map(move|r|(t.clone(),r.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>(),NOW).map_err(|e|format!("generated snapshot rejected by schema: {e}"))?;
    let actual=readback(&engine)?;
    for (name,values) in &actual {for row in values {
        snapback4_core::engine::validate::validate_row(&bound.schema,name,row).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
        for c in &bound.schema.tables[name].constraints {if let Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{table,index,key}=c {
            let rule=RuleExpr::Exists{table:table.clone(),index:index.clone(),key:key.clone(),provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed,equalities:Vec::new()};
            if engine.fixture_policy(&rule,snapback4_core::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL,None,row,NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=rules::Verdict::Readable {return Err(format!("{name}: trusted seed did not establish final-exists constraint"));}
        }}
    }}
    Ok(engine)
}
fn replay(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,viewer:&str,word:&str,row:&Value)->Result<(bool,Value),String>{
    let name="$security.probe";let table=inv.table.clone();let key=id(&table);
    let read=Read{site:"security challenge".into(),table:table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),key:vec![Expr::Literal(key.clone())],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed};
    let mut seeds=rows.clone();
    let probe=match word{
        "read"=>program(name,ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Get(read))]),
        "insert"=>{seeds.remove(&table);program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Insert{table,row:Expr::Literal(row.clone())},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])},
        "delete"=>program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Delete{table,key:Expr::Literal(key)},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))]),
        "update"=>{
            let definition=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
            let changed=definition.columns.iter().filter(|(n,_)|n.as_str()!="id"&&!definition.engine_field(n))
                .filter(|(n,_)|inv.column.as_ref().is_none_or(|c|c==*n))
                .filter(|(n,_)|!definition.constraints.iter().any(|c|matches!(c,Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(n))))
                .find_map(|(n,k)| {let old=row.field(n).ok()?;let next=match (k.required(),old){
                    (C::Bool,Value::Bool(v))=>Value::Bool(!v),
                    (C::Enum{variants},_)=>Value::String(variants.iter().find(|v|Some(Value::String((*v).clone()))!=Some(old.clone()))?.clone()),
                    (C::Text{max},Value::String(v)) if v.chars().count()<*max as usize=>Value::String(format!("{v}x")),
                    (C::Int|C::Time,Value::Int(v))=>Value::Int(v+1),_=>return None};
                    Some((n.clone(),next))});
            let Some((column,value))=changed else{return Err("no valid changed mutable-column sample".into());};
            program(name,ProgramKind::Mutation,vec![Stmt::Update{table,key:Expr::Literal(key),patch:Expr::Literal(Value::Map([(column,value)].into_iter().collect()))},Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])
        },_=>return Err("row integrity/transition challenge is not an operation admission test".into()),
    };
    let mut engine=engine(bound,&seeds,probe)?;
    if word=="read"{return engine.query(name,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map(|q|(q.data!=Value::Null,row.clone())).map_err(|e|e.to_string());}
    let outcome=engine.mutate(name,None,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(NOW)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
    if !matches!(outcome.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..}){return Ok((false,row.clone()));}
    let next=engine.store().rows(&inv.table).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.into_iter().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==row.field("id").ok());
    if word=="delete"{return if next.is_none(){Ok((true,row.clone()))}else{Err("delete returned sent without removing the target".into())};}
    let next=next.ok_or("sent write has no committed target image")?;
    if word=="update" {
        let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
        let changed=table.columns.keys().filter(|c|!table.engine_field(c)&&!table.maintained.contains_key(*c))
            .any(|c|row.field(c).ok()!=next.field(c).ok()&&inv.column.as_ref().is_none_or(|column|column==c));
        if !changed{return Err("sent no-op/implicit-only update has no changed declared target column".into());}
    }
    Ok((true,next))
}
fn readback(engine:&Engine)->Result<BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,String>{engine.schema().tables.keys().map(|t|engine.store().rows(t).map(|rs|(t.clone(),rs)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())).collect()}

fn operation_preflight(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<(),String>{
    let Some(name)=&inv.operation else{return Ok(());};
    let p=&bound.programs.iter().find(|p|&p.program.name==name).ok_or("operation did not bind")?.program;
    if !super::census::body(&p.body){return Err(format!("{name}: opaque/loop/scan path requires write-boundary tracing"));}
    let rule=inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("operation policy outside rule fragment")?;
    if rule.any_term(&|t|!matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))){return Err("operation policy requires write-boundary row-image tracing".into());}
    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
    if writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table).count()>1{return Err(format!("{name}: multiple target writes need executed-effect tracing; net images do not identify delete/reinsert"));}
    let unstable=kernel::dependencies(rule).iter().any(|dep|{
        let hits=writes.iter().filter(|(t,_)|t==dep).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        !hits.is_empty()&&!(dep==&inv.table&&hits.len()==1&&hits[0].1=="insert")

//! Real upload ledgers for foreign-id and non-image placement challenges.
// @ref LLP 3000.038#3-upload — assets retain upload ownership and verified media type.
use super::*;
const PDF:&[u8]=b"%PDF-1.4\n%%EOF\n";
pub(super) fn file(e:&mut Engine,owner:&str,types:&[String])->Result<Value,String>{
    let mime=types.first().map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("application/pdf");
    let (bytes,name)=match mime{"application/pdf"=>(PDF,"proof.pdf"),"text/plain"=>(b"verified text\n".as_slice(),"proof.txt"),"image/png"=>(PNG.as_slice(),"proof.png"),_=>return Err(format!("file ledger needs a verified {mime} fixture"))};
    e.store_file(owner,bytes,Some(mime),name,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())
}
fn contains(value:&Value,id:&Value)->bool {
    if value==id{return true;}
    match value{Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|contains(v,id)),Value::Map(m)=>m.values().any(|v|contains(v,id)),Value::Row(m)=>m.values().any(|v|contains(v,id)),_=>false}
}
pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    let Ok(initial)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    if initial.values().any(|xs|!xs.is_empty()){
        out.unsupported.insert("foreign-asset replay over populated fixtures needs full ledger transfer; empty-ledger challenges run separately".into());
    }
    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native){
        let p=&bp.program;
        for (arg,kind) in &p.args{
            if !matches!(kind,ir::ArgType::Image{..}|ir::ArgType::File{..}){continue;}
            for mode in ["foreign upload","non-image photo"]{
                if mode=="non-image photo"&&!matches!(kind,ir::ArgType::Image{..}){continue;}
                let mut e=match engine(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),program("$security.asset",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(error);continue;}};
                let sample=if mode=="non-image photo"{file(&mut e,VIEWER,&[])}else{match kind{
                    ir::ArgType::Image{..}=>e.store_asset(OTHER,&PNG,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|e.to_string()),
                    ir::ArgType::File{types,..}=>file(&mut e,OTHER,types),_=>unreachable!(),
                }};
                let sample=match sample{Ok(v)=>v,Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(error);continue;}};
                let Some(mut args)=arguments(&p.args,0)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: asset challenge needs remaining argument fixtures",p.name));continue;};
                args.insert(arg.clone(),sample.clone());
                out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
                match e.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(VIEWER,args).at(NOW)){
                    Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..})=>out.refused+=1,
                    Ok(_)=>{if let Ok(rows)=readback(&e)&&rows.values().flatten().any(|row|contains(row,&sample)){
                        out.boundary_failures.push(format!("{} placed {mode} through argument {arg}",p.name));
                    }},
                    Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: asset challenge engine error {error}",p.name));},
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
//! Exercise actual logical lifetime reads between deadlines and cleanup ticks.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#102-temporal-intent — no snapshot-only clock refutation.
use super::*;

pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(super::super::temporal::has_clock)
        && sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))) {
        let policy=inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap();
        if policy.has_reference(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: temporal reference policy needs authorized per-tick engine replay",inv.table));continue;}
        if !kernel::dependencies(policy).is_empty(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: clock policy with raw-probe dependencies needs a live-snapshot evaluator",inv.table));continue;}
        let name="$security.temporal";
        let probe=program(name,ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Get(Read{site:"temporal security".into(),table:inv.table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),
            key:vec![Expr::Literal(id(&inv.table))],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed}))]);
        for variant in [0,4] {
            let rows=match snapshot_mode(bound,inv,variant,true){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
            for now in [NOW,NOW+999,NOW+1000,NOW+1001,NOW+1999,NOW+2000,NOW+2001] {
                // Recreate the store for each boundary; never sweep before the
                // read. Unswept expiry remains a logical absence by contract.
                let mut engine=match engine(bound,&rows,probe.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
                let actual=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
                let Some(row)=actual.get(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.first())else{continue;};
                for viewer in [VIEWER,OTHER] {
                    out.attempted+=1;
                    let Ok(q)=engine.query(name,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(now))else{continue;};
                    if q.unavailable||!q.complete||q.data==Value::Null{continue;}
                    // Scalar clock atoms are evaluated only after a real
                    // engine read returned this committed row. This extra
                    // field is proof-private and never enters the store.
                    let mut image=row.clone();
                    if let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image {fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(now));}
                    if matches!(rules::evaluate(policy,viewer,None,&image,false,&mut Snapshot{schema:&bound.schema,rows:&actual}),Ok(false)) {
                        out.admitted+=1;
                        out.violations.push(Counterexample {reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:name.into(),
                            effect:"read".into(),row:row.clone(),old:None,supporting_rows:actual.clone(),rule_path:format!("server read at {now}, before any physical sweep; clock policy false"),
                            scope:"actual engine read over schema-valid committed seed ingress at the recorded server clock; schedules are not assumed to have run".into()});
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

    /// `Store::memory()` created one and nothing removed it — one empty
    /// directory per in-memory engine, four million of them in a week of test
    /// runs on one machine, until listing the temp directory took minutes.
    ephemeral_assets: bool,
    identity: activation::Identity,
    #[cfg(test)]
    full_adoption: bool,
    pub adoption_report: adoption_report::AdoptionReport,
}

impl Drop for Store {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        if self.ephemeral_assets {
            let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.assets_dir);
        }
    }
}

/// One transaction over the store, with the catalog in reach.

    activation_scan: Option<activation_scan::Buffer>,
    pub changes: std::cell::RefCell<crate::revocation::Recorder>,
    sequence_table: std::cell::Cell<Option<bool>>,
    tx: rusqlite::Transaction<'a>,
    page_size: u64,
    server: bool,

 succeeded in 0ms:
        // beside the file itself, so every release serves the same bytes.
        let real = std::fs::canonicalize(path).unwrap_or_else(|_| path.to_path_buf());
        let assets_dir = real.parent().map(|dir| dir.join("assets")).unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("assets"));
        let store = Self::prepare(conn, assets_dir, false, server)?;
        for suffix in ["-wal", "-shm", "-journal"] {
            crate::private_fs::existing_file(&PathBuf::from(format!("{}{suffix}", real.display())))
                .map_err(io)?;
        }
        Ok(store)
    }

    pub fn memory() -> Result<Self, RuntimeError> {
        let assets_dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
            "snapback4-{}-{}", std::process::id(), crate::ids::mint(crate::now_ms(), &mut crate::ids::Mint::default())?
        ));
        Self::prepare(Connection::open_in_memory()?, assets_dir, true, true)
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.023#12 — create server metadata at open, before reads can
    // cache plans. DDL during a commit would invalidate those prepared reads.
    fn initialize_change_evidence(conn: &Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        conn.execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _change_evidence (seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, footprint TEXT NOT NULL)")?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Where this store keeps uploaded bytes (`assets/<id>`).
    pub fn assets_dir(&self) -> &Path {
        &self.assets_dir
    }

    /// Whether the assets directory belongs to this store alone (a store in
    /// memory) rather than to the project beside the database.
    pub fn ephemeral_assets(&self) -> bool {
        self.ephemeral_assets
    }

    fn write_row(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, fields: &[ObjectField], current: Option<&str>, site: &Site, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        objects::explicit_fields(fields.iter().filter(|f| !f.spread).map(|f| (f.name.as_str(), &f.site)))?;
        let expanded;
        let fields = if fields.iter().any(|field| field.spread) {
            expanded = self.expand_object_fields(fields, Some(info), out)?;
            &expanded
        } else { fields };
        for field in fields {
            if super::columns::fields(info).any(|name|name == field.name) {
                return Err(super::columns::refusal(&field.site,format!("{}.{} is engine-maintained; write its source rows instead",info.decl.name,field.name)));
            }
        }
        match current {
            None => {
                if fields.iter().any(|f| f.name == "id") {
                    let (given, ty) = fields.iter().find(|f| f.name == "id").map(|f| f.value.as_ref().map(|v| self.expr_as_reference(v, out, &Ty::Id)).unwrap_or_else(|| self.name("id", site))).unwrap()?;
                    if !ty.assignable_to(&Ty::Id) { return Err(type_error(site,format!("an inserted id must be an id, got {}",ty.name()),"use row.id for a row snapshot; reference columns accept a required same-table row")); }
                    return self.write_row_with_id(info, &fields.iter().filter(|f| f.name != "id").cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>(), given, site, out);
                }
                self.write_row_with_id(info, fields, Expr::Input(Input::NewId), site, out)
            }
            Some(_) => {
                // A patch: only the named columns, each typed.
                let mut exprs = Vec::<(String, Expr)>::new();
                let mut types = BTreeMap::new();
                for field in fields {
                    if field.name == "id" {
                        return Err(unknown(&field.site, "update cannot change id", "remove id from the patch"));
                    }
                    let Some(scalar) = info.columns.get(&field.name) else {
                        return Err(unknown(&field.site, format!("{} has no column {}", info.decl.name, field.name), name_rewrite(&field.name, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str), None)));
                    };
                    let expected = Ty::from_scalar(scalar);
                    let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
                    let (e, t) = self.field_value(field, &expected, &mut setup)?;
                    literal_in_range(info, &field.name, scalar, &e, &field.site)?;
                    self.sequence_setup(exprs.iter_mut().map(|(_, e)| e), setup, out);
                    if !t.writable_to(&expected) {
                        return Err(diagnostics::write_field_type(info, field, &expected, &t));
                    }
                    let e = if expr::allocates(&e) { self.snapshot(e, out) } else { e };
                    exprs.push((field.name.clone(), e));
                    if types.insert(field.name.clone(), t).is_some() {
                        return Err(unknown(&field.site, format!("field {} is written twice", field.name), "keep one"));
                    }
                }
                Ok((Expr::Object(exprs.into_iter().collect()), Ty::Object(types)))
            }
        }
    }

//! Exercise actual logical lifetime reads between deadlines and cleanup ticks.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#102-temporal-intent — no snapshot-only clock refutation.
use super::*;

pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign,sites:Option<&BTreeSet<String>>){
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&i.column.is_none()&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(super::super::temporal::has_clock)
        && sites.is_none_or(|s|s.contains(&i.site.to_string()))) {
        let policy=inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap();
        if policy.has_reference(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: temporal reference policy needs authorized per-tick engine replay",inv.table));continue;}
        if !kernel::dependencies(policy).is_empty(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: clock policy with raw-probe dependencies needs a live-snapshot evaluator",inv.table));continue;}
        let name="$security.temporal";
        let probe=program(name,ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Get(Read{site:"temporal security".into(),table:inv.table.clone(),index:"byId".into(),
            key:vec![Expr::Literal(id(&inv.table))],provenance:KeyProvenance::Computed}))]);
        for variant in [0,4] {
            let rows=match snapshot_mode(bound,inv,variant,true){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
            for now in [NOW,NOW+999,NOW+1000,NOW+1001,NOW+1999,NOW+2000,NOW+2001] {
                // Recreate the store for each boundary; never sweep before the
                // read. Unswept expiry remains a logical absence by contract.
                let mut engine=match engine(bound,&rows,probe.clone()){Ok(e)=>e,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
                let actual=match readback(&engine){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);continue;}};
                let Some(row)=actual.get(&inv.table).and_then(|rs|rs.first())else{continue;};
                for viewer in [VIEWER,OTHER] {
                    out.attempted+=1;
                    let Ok(q)=engine.query(name,Context::new(viewer,BTreeMap::new()).at(now))else{continue;};
                    if q.unavailable||!q.complete||q.data==Value::Null{continue;}
                    // Scalar clock atoms are evaluated only after a real
                    // engine read returned this committed row. This extra
                    // field is proof-private and never enters the store.
                    let mut image=row.clone();
                    if let Value::Row(fields)=&mut image {fields.insert("$security:server-clock".into(),Value::Int(now));}
                    if matches!(rules::evaluate(policy,viewer,None,&image,false,&mut Snapshot{schema:&bound.schema,rows:&actual}),Ok(false)) {
                        out.admitted+=1;
                        out.violations.push(Counterexample {reachability:Reachability::SyntheticOperation,history:Vec::new(),observations:None,invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:name.into(),
                            effect:"read".into(),row:row.clone(),old:None,supporting_rows:actual.clone(),rule_path:format!("server read at {now}, before any physical sweep; clock policy false"),
                            scope:"actual engine read over schema-valid committed seed ingress at the recorded server clock; schedules are not assumed to have run".into()});
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

//! Expanded helpers can write from any expression block.
// @ref LLP 3000.022#14.3 — legacy eligibility examines the complete expanded write footprint.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn stmts_write_tables(stmts: &[Stmt], out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    for stmt in stmts {
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Insert { table, .. } | Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "insert")),
            Stmt::Update { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "update")),
            Stmt::Upsert { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "upsert")),
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, upsert, .. } => out.push((target.table.clone(), if *upsert { "upsert" } else { "update" })),
            Stmt::Delete { table, .. } => out.push((table.clone(), "delete")),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => out.push((target.table.clone(), "delete")),
            _ => {}
        }
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Const { value, .. } | Stmt::Let { value, .. } | Stmt::Assign { value, .. } | Stmt::Push { value, .. } | Stmt::Return(value) => expr_write_tables(value, out),
            Stmt::Emit { payload: value, once, .. } => { expr_write_tables(value, out); if let Some(value) = once { expr_write_tables(value, out); } },
            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body } => {
                expr_write_tables(condition, out);
                stmts_write_tables(then_body, out);
                stmts_write_tables(else_body, out);
            }
            Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => {
                match domain {
                    LoopDomain::Page(scan) => scan_write_tables(scan, out),
                    LoopDomain::ExpressionRange { start, end_exclusive } => { expr_write_tables(start, out); expr_write_tables(end_exclusive, out); }
                    LoopDomain::DerivedArray { value, .. } => expr_write_tables(value, out),
                    LoopDomain::Recurse(recurse) => {
                        expr_write_tables(&recurse.seed, out);
                    }
                    LoopDomain::LiteralRange { .. } | LoopDomain::ArgList { .. } => {}
                }
                stmts_write_tables(body, out);
            }
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expr_write_tables(condition, out),
            Stmt::Check { condition, current } => {
                expr_write_tables(condition, out);
                for key in &current.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
            }
            Stmt::InsertIfAbsent { row, .. } | Stmt::Insert { row, .. } => expr_write_tables(row, out),
            Stmt::Update { key, patch, .. } => {
                expr_write_tables(key, out);
                expr_write_tables(patch, out);
            }
            Stmt::Upsert { key, row, .. } => {
                expr_write_tables(key, out);
                expr_write_tables(row, out);
            }
            Stmt::WriteTarget { target, patch, condition, .. } => {
                for key in &target.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
                expr_write_tables(patch, out);
                if let Some(condition) = condition { stmts_write_tables(&condition.setup, out); expr_write_tables(&condition.value, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Delete { key, .. } => expr_write_tables(key, out),
            Stmt::DeleteTarget { target } => {
                for key in &target.key { expr_write_tables(key, out); }
            }
            Stmt::Break | Stmt::Continue => {}
        }
    }
}

fn scan_write_tables(scan: &Scan, out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    if let Some(filter) = &scan.filter { expr_write_tables(filter, out); }
    if let Some(range) = &scan.range {
        for bound in [&range.gt, &range.gte, &range.lt, &range.lte].into_iter().flatten() { expr_write_tables(bound, out); }
    }
    for key in &scan.prefix {
        match key { IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(expr) | IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(expr) => expr_write_tables(expr, out) }
    }
    for cursor in [&scan.after, &scan.before].into_iter().flatten() { expr_write_tables(cursor, out); }
}

fn expr_write_tables(expr: &Expr, out: &mut Vec<(String, &'static str)>) {
    match expr {
        Expr::Literal(_) | Expr::Variable(_) | Expr::Input(_) => {}
        Expr::Field { base, .. } => expr_write_tables(base, out),
        Expr::Array(items) => items.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Object(fields) => fields.values().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Shape { base, fields } => { expr_write_tables(base, out); fields.values().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)); }
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => {
            expr_write_tables(left, out);
            expr_write_tables(right, out);
        }
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } => expr_write_tables(value, out),
        Expr::Builtin { args, .. } => args.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out)),
        Expr::Get(read) | Expr::Exists(read) => {
            read.key.iter().for_each(|item| expr_write_tables(item, out));
        }
        Expr::Block { body, value } => { stmts_write_tables(body, out); expr_write_tables(value, out); }
        Expr::Scan(scan) => scan_write_tables(scan, out),
        Expr::Merge(merge) => {
            if let Some(after) = merge.position.as_ref().and_then(|position| position.after.as_ref()) { expr_write_tables(after, out); }
            for lane in &merge.lanes {
                if let Some(guard) = &lane.guard { expr_write_tables(guard, out); }
                if let Some(project) = &lane.project { expr_write_tables(project, out); }
                if let Some(value) = &lane.distinct { expr_write_tables(value, out); }
                stmts_write_tables(&lane.setup, out);
                if let Some(source) = &lane.source {
                    if let Some(scan) = source.scan() { scan_write_tables(scan, out); }
                    for value in source.expressions() { expr_write_tables(value, out); }
                }
                scan_write_tables(&lane.scan, out);
            }
        }
    }
}

//! Typed server-clock facts used only by the proof abstraction, never runtime rules.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#102-temporal-intent — lifetime is independent of physical sweep lag.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::{Value, ir::BinaryOp as Op, schema::{Table, ColumnType}};
// Quarry identifiers cannot contain ':'. No authored field aliases this atom.
const CLOCK: &str = "$security:server-clock";
pub(super) fn clock() -> RuleTerm { RuleTerm::Field(CLOCK.into()) }
pub(super) fn has_clock(rule: &RuleExpr) -> bool {
    rule.any_term(&|t| matches!(t, RuleTerm::Field(f) if f == CLOCK))
}
pub(super) fn comparison(op: Op, a: RuleTerm, b: RuleTerm) -> RuleExpr {
    match op {
        Op::Gt => RuleExpr::Compare(Op::Lt,b,a), Op::Gte => RuleExpr::Compare(Op::Lte,b,a),
        Op::Eq => RuleExpr::Eq(a,b), Op::Ne => RuleExpr::Ne(a,b),
        _ => RuleExpr::Compare(op,a,b),
    }
}
pub(super) fn admission(table: &Table) -> RuleExpr {
    let mut facts = vec![table.rules.read.clone()];
    for (column,op) in table.not_before.iter().map(|f|(f,Op::Lte)).chain(table.expire.iter().map(|f|(f,Op::Gt))) {
        let field=RuleTerm::Field(column.clone());
        let edge=comparison(op,field.clone(),clock());
        let edge=if table.columns.get(column).is_some_and(ColumnType::is_optional) {
            RuleExpr::Or(vec![RuleExpr::Eq(field,RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null)),edge])
        } else {edge};
        facts.push(edge);
    }
    RuleExpr::And(facts)
}

pub(super) fn bind(catalog: &Catalog, info: &super::super::catalog::TableInfo, expr: &ast::Expr) -> Option<RuleExpr> {
    use ast::{Expr,Literal,TypeKind};
    fn term(info:&super::super::catalog::TableInfo,e:&Expr)->Option<RuleTerm>{match e {
        Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now" => Some(clock()),
        Expr::Member{base,name,..} if matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Focal{..}) && info.columns.get(name).is_some_and(|t|t.kind==TypeKind::Time) => Some(RuleTerm::Field(name.clone())),
        Expr::Literal{value:Literal::Number(n),..}=>Some(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Int(n.parse().ok()?))),
        Expr::Literal{value:Literal::Null,..}=>Some(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null)),
        _=>None,
    }}
    if let Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=expr {
        if op=="and"||op=="or" {
            let xs=vec![bind(catalog,info,left)?,bind(catalog,info,right)?];
            return Some(if op=="and"{RuleExpr::And(xs)}else{RuleExpr::Or(xs)});
        }
        let op=match op.as_str(){"="=>Op::Eq,"!="=>Op::Ne,"<"=>Op::Lt,"<="=>Op::Lte,">"=>Op::Gt,">="=>Op::Gte,_=>return None};
        if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(term(info,left),term(info,right)){return Some(comparison(op,a,b));}
    }
    if let Expr::Unary{op,value,..}=expr && op=="not" {return Some(RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(bind(catalog,info,value)?)));}
    let rule=ast::RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Insert,bindings:Vec::new(),value:expr.clone(),site:expr.site().clone()};
    super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&rule).ok()
}
//! Author-owned intent starts with a reviewable draft, never inferred policy.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#103-adoption — syntactic coverage is distinct from a proved invariant.
use super::*;

pub(super) fn missing(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
    bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(name,t)| {
        ([&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|!matches!(r,RuleExpr::Deny)) || !t.written_by.is_empty())
            && !bound.invariants.iter().any(|i| &i.table==*name)
    }).map(|(name,_)|name.clone()).collect()
}

/// Suggested declarations mirror enforcement, not the product specification.
/// The caller must keep this outside the compiled .q source set.
pub fn draft(bound:&Bound)->String{
    let mut out=String::from("-- DRAFT: NOT ACCEPTED. Generated from access rules, not the app specification.\n-- Confirm or correct each policy against the spec, then copy chosen declarations\n-- into an authored snapback/*.q file. This .q.draft file is never compiled.\n-- Check current roles, grant revocation, count/existence privacy, deadlines and\n-- required evidence separately. A copied wrong rule cannot catch itself.\n-- snapback4 guide security\n\n");
    for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
        for (effect,rule) in [("read",&t.rules.read),("insert",&t.rules.insert),("update",&t.rules.update),("delete",&t.rules.delete)] {
            if matches!(rule,RuleExpr::Deny)||covered(bound,name,effect){continue;}
            let predicate=if matches!(rule,RuleExpr::Public{..}){"allow".into()}else{rule.quarry()};
            if predicate.contains('$') || effect=="update" && t.update_transition {
                out.push_str(&format!("-- {name}.{effect}: write intent explicitly; captured/transition rule: {predicate}\n"));
            }else {out.push_str(&format!("-- Candidate from {name} {effect} rule; AUTHOR MUST CONFIRM.\ninvariant {name}.{effect} only {predicate}\n"));}
        }
        for (column,authority) in &t.written_by {
            out.push_str(&format!("-- {name}.{column}: effective field-owner patch authority .{} = viewer bypasses the table update rule; declare column-write intent from the spec.\n",authority.owner));
        }
    }
    out
}
fn covered(bound:&Bound,table:&str,effect:&str)->bool{
    bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==table && i.operation.is_none() && i.column.is_none()
        && (i.target==effect || i.target=="write" && matches!(effect,"insert"|"update")))
}
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct UncoveredExposure {pub table:String,pub effect:String,pub audience:String,pub row_predicate:String,pub column:Option<String>}
pub(super) fn uncovered(bound:&Bound,exposures:&[Exposure])->Vec<UncoveredExposure>{
    let mut out=exposures.iter().flat_map(|x|x.effects.iter().filter(|(effect,rule)|rule.as_str()!="deny"&&!covered(bound,&x.table,effect)).map(|(effect,predicate)|UncoveredExposure{
        table:x.table.clone(),effect:effect.clone(),audience:if predicate=="allow"||predicate.starts_with("public"){"every signed-in user".into()}else{format!("caller satisfying {predicate}")},row_predicate:predicate.clone(),column:None,
    })).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){for (column,authority) in &t.written_by{
        if covered(bound,name,"update")||bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==*name&&i.operation.is_none()&&i.column.as_ref()==Some(column)&&i.target=="write"){continue;}
        out.push(UncoveredExposure{table:name.clone(),effect:"update".into(),column:Some(column.clone()),audience:format!("owner .{} = viewer",authority.owner),row_predicate:format!("field-owner patch authority for {column}; table update rule may deny")});
    }}out
}

//! Field authority and projection share one schema fact on every host.
// @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — the owner is a principal on this row, not a row update grant.
use super::*;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct FieldAuthority {
    pub owner: String,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "std::ops::Not::not")]
    pub audience: bool,
}

impl Table {
    pub fn private_field(&self, field: &str) -> bool {
        self.written_by.get(field).is_some_and(|authority| !authority.audience)
    }

    pub fn field_owners(&self, row: &Value) -> BTreeSet<String> {
        self.written_by.values().filter(|a| !a.audience).filter_map(|a|
            row.field(&a.owner).ok().and_then(|v| v.as_str().ok()).map(str::to_owned)).collect()
    }
}

// Private values cannot enter an index key (including cursors), an audience,
// attribution, or a materialized aggregate's ordinary readable output. This
// also checks resolved/inferred indexes and raw schemas, not just syntax.
pub fn validate(schema: &Schema) -> Vec<SchemaIssue> {
    let mut issues = Vec::new();
    let mut issue = |name: &str, field: &str, why: String| issues.push(SchemaIssue {
        path: format!("tables.{name}.columns.{field}"), message: format!("written by: {name}.{field} {why}") });
    for (name, table) in &schema.tables {
        for (field, authority) in &table.written_by {
            if !table.columns.contains_key(field) || field == "id" || table.columns.get(&authority.owner) != Some(&ColumnType::Principal)
                || table.written_by.contains_key(&authority.owner) || table.maintained.contains_key(field) || table.recorded.contains_key(field) {
                issue(name, field, format!("needs an ordinary required principal owner column .{} on the same table", authority.owner));
            }
            if !table.defaults.contains_key(field) {
                issue(name, field, "needs a declared default for inserts by other principals".into());
            }
            if authority.audience { continue; }
            if table.indexes.values().any(|index| index.uses_column(field)) || table.expire.as_ref() == Some(field) {
                issue(name, field, "is private and cannot supply an index/cursor or lifetime; declare readable by audience to use it there".into());
            }
            for timeline in &schema.timelines {
                let mut dependencies = BTreeSet::new();
                for mapping in [&timeline.authors[..], &timeline.posts[..], &timeline.reposts[..], &timeline.edges[..]] {
                    if mapping[0] == *name { dependencies.extend(mapping[1..].iter().cloned()); }
                }
                if timeline.posts[0] == *name && let Some(filter) = &timeline.filter { filter.fields(&mut dependencies); }
                if dependencies.contains(field) {
                    issue(name, field, "is private and cannot supply a timeline mapping or predicate; declare readable by audience first".into());
                }
            }
            for maintain in &schema.maintains {
                if let Some(key) = &maintain.row_key
                    && key.table == *name && key.fields().contains(field)
                    && !requires_owner(&table.rules.read, &authority.owner) {
                    issue(name, field, "is private and can feed a row-keyed count only when the member read rule requires its owner; restrict the member audience or declare readable by audience".into());
                }
                if maintain.source == *name && maintain.dependencies().contains(field) {
                    issue(name, field, "is private and cannot feed a readable aggregate; declare readable by audience first".into());
                }
            }
        }
        for (other_name, other) in &schema.tables {
            for capture in other.recorded.values() {
                if capture.table == *name && table.private_field(&capture.column) { issue(other_name, &capture.column, "cannot record another row's private field".into()); }
            }
        }
        for (effect, rule) in [("read", &table.rules.read), ("insert", &table.rules.insert), ("update", &table.rules.update), ("delete", &table.rules.delete)] {
            private_rule(schema, name, rule, effect == "update" && table.update_transition, &mut issue);
        }
    }
    issues
}

// Every admitted member reader must also be allowed to see the predicate input.
// A conjunctive owner grant proves that; an alternative grant does not.
fn requires_owner(rule: &RuleExpr, owner: &str) -> bool {
    match rule {
        RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(field), RuleTerm::Viewer)
        | RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer, RuleTerm::Field(field)) => field == owner,
        RuleExpr::And(parts) => parts.iter().any(|part| requires_owner(part, owner)),
        _ => false,
    }
}

fn private_rule(schema: &Schema, name: &str, rule: &RuleExpr, update: bool, issue: &mut impl FnMut(&str, &str, String)) {
    let term = |term: &RuleTerm, issue: &mut dyn FnMut(&str, &str, String)| {
        if !update && let RuleTerm::Field(field) | RuleTerm::OldField(field) | RuleTerm::NextField(field) = term
            && schema.tables[name].private_field(field) {
            issue(name, field, "is private; only a named update (old, next) transition may use it as a rule input; declare readable by audience for other rules".into());
        }
    };
    match rule {
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b) | RuleExpr::Ne(a,b) | RuleExpr::Compare(_,a,b) => { term(a,issue); term(b,issue); }
        RuleExpr::And(items) | RuleExpr::Or(items) => for item in items { private_rule(schema,name,item,update,issue); },
        RuleExpr::Not(inner) => private_rule(schema,name,inner,update,issue),
        RuleExpr::Exists { table, key, equalities, .. } | RuleExpr::Created { table, key, equalities, .. } => {
            for item in key { term(item,issue); }
            for (field,value) in equalities {
                term(value,issue);
                if schema.table(table).is_some_and(|t|t.private_field(field)) { issue(table,field,"is private and cannot be probed from a rule".into()); }
            }
        }
        _ => {}
    }
}

impl RuleExpr {
    /// Comparing an untouched field with itself needs no knowledge of its
    /// value. Every other use (including a probe key) requires field visibility.
    pub fn needs_field_value(&self, field: &str, kind: &ColumnType) -> bool {
        let term = |term: &RuleTerm| matches!(term, RuleTerm::Field(name) | RuleTerm::OldField(name) | RuleTerm::NextField(name) if name == field);
        match self {
            Self::Eq(a,b) | Self::Ne(a,b) => term(a) != term(b),

 succeeded in 0ms:
            return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "insert rule") });
        }
        if !table.sequences.is_empty() {
            // Admission reserves the worst case before either read verdict;
            // restoring a refusal alone cannot hide a boundary abort.
            // An independent insert rule was already prepaid and evaluated.
            // Only still-pending checks belong in this preflight allowance.
            let insert_checks = if authorized { 0 } else { 1 + u64::from(upsert_index.is_some() && supplied_sequence.is_ok()) };
            self.reserve_sequence_authorization(table, &row, insert_checks)?;
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.015#2 — an unreadable allocation cannot expose counter,
        // uniqueness or cap state, even to a principal allowed to insert.
        if !self.sequence_insert_readable(table_name, &row)? {
            self.refusing_clause(table_name, "sequence read authority");
            return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "sequence read authority") });
        }
        if let Err(error) = supplied_sequence {
            // @ref LLP 3000.007#17 — absent upserts decide insert authority before field diagnostics.
            // A target key may supply an ordinal even when the patch does not.
            if !authorized && !self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Insert, None, &row)? {
                return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { self.rule_refusal(table_name, Effect::Insert, "insert rule") });
            }
            return Err(if upsert_index.is_some() { self.no_write_target() } else { error });
        }
        if upsert_index.is_some() {
            self.authorize_fields(table_name, &row, &supplied).map_err(|_| self.no_write_target())?;
        } else { self.authorize_fields(table_name, &row, &supplied)?; }
        // The image copy an event slot costs is the writer's own supplied bytes (LLP 3000.015 §1).
        let image_bytes = row.heap_bytes();
        let (row, sequence_full) = self.sequence_fields(table_name, row)?;
        let row = self.receipt_insert_fields(table_name, row)?;
        if let Some(index) = upsert_index {
            if self.device { self.prove_upsert_insert(table_name, index, &row)?; }
            // Preserve upsert's target preflight before the ordinary insert checks;
            // only this first authority refusal is an undisclosed target miss.
            if !authorized && !self.check_rule(table_name, Effect::Insert, None, &row)? { return Err(self.no_write_target()); }

                out.insert(format!("{} may copy protected data into another table; cross-table disclosure reduction is unproved",p.program.name));

            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && audit::authored_refusal(&json) {out.insert(format!("{}: authored refusal observations are not reduced to this read policy",p.program.name));}
        }
    }
    out.into_iter().collect()
}

pub fn add_source_notes(report:&mut Report,sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound){
    report.notes.extend(lints::source_notes(sources,bound));
    let mut sites=BTreeMap::new();
    for (file,source) in sources{let (doc,_)=crate::parse_document(file,source);for t in doc.tables{
        sites.insert(t.name.clone(),t.site.clone());for c in t.columns{sites.insert(format!("{}.{}",t.name,c.name),c.site);}
        for r in t.rules{sites.insert(format!("{}.{}",t.name,r.effect.word()),r.site);}
    }}
    for note in &mut report.notes{if note.site.line==0 {
        let key=note.message.split([' ',':']).next().unwrap_or("");
        note.site=sites.get(key).or_else(||sites.get(key.split('.').next().unwrap_or(""))).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Site::new(sources.first().map(|s|s.0.as_str()).unwrap_or("schema.q"),1,1));
    }}
}

pub(super) fn file(e:&mut Engine,owner:&str,types:&[String])->Result<Value,String>{
    let mime=types.first().map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("application/pdf");
    let (bytes,name)=match mime{"application/pdf"=>(PDF,"proof.pdf"),"text/plain"=>(b"verified text\n".as_slice(),"proof.txt"),"image/png"=>(PNG.as_slice(),"proof.png"),_=>return Err(format!("file ledger needs a verified {mime} fixture"))};

}
fn contains(value:&Value,id:&Value)->bool {
    if value==id{return true;}
    match value{Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|contains(v,id)),Value::Map(m)=>m.values().any(|v|contains(v,id)),Value::Row(m)=>m.values().any(|v|contains(v,id)),_=>false}
}
pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    let Ok(initial)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    if initial.values().any(|xs|!xs.is_empty()){
        out.unsupported.insert("foreign-asset replay over populated fixtures needs full ledger transfer; empty-ledger challenges run separately".into());
    }

        let p=&bp.program;

            if !matches!(kind,ir::ArgType::Image{..}|ir::ArgType::File{..}){continue;}
            for mode in ["foreign upload","non-image photo"]{
                if mode=="non-image photo"&&!matches!(kind,ir::ArgType::Image{..}){continue;}
                let mut e=match engine(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),program("$security.asset",ProgramKind::Query,vec![Stmt::Return(Expr::Literal(Value::Null))])){Ok(e)=>e,Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(error);continue;}};

                    ir::ArgType::Image{..}=>e.store_asset(OTHER,&PNG,NOW).map(|a|a.column_value()).map_err(|e|e.to_string()),
                    ir::ArgType::File{types,..}=>file(&mut e,OTHER,types),_=>unreachable!(),
                }};
                let sample=match sample{Ok(v)=>v,Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(error);continue;}};
                let Some(mut args)=arguments(&p.args,0)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: asset challenge needs remaining argument fixtures",p.name));continue;};
                args.insert(arg.clone(),sample.clone());
                out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
                match e.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(VIEWER,args).at(NOW)){
                    Ok(result) if matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed{..})=>out.refused+=1,
                    Ok(_)=>{if let Ok(rows)=readback(&e)&&rows.values().flatten().any(|row|contains(row,&sample)){
                        out.boundary_failures.push(format!("{} placed {mode} through argument {arg}",p.name));
                    }},
                    Err(error)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: asset challenge engine error {error}",p.name));},
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// The disjuncts of the rule: an `or` flattens, anything else is itself.
    pub fn disjuncts(&self) -> Vec<&RuleExpr> {
        match self {
            Self::Or(items) => items.iter().flat_map(|item| item.disjuncts()).collect(),
            other => vec![other],
        }
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.6 — service seats read on the server and never enlarge a device's audience.
    pub fn service_grant(&self) -> Option<&str> {
        match self {
            Self::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer, RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Id(principal)))
            | Self::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Id(principal)), RuleTerm::Viewer)
                if principal.strip_prefix("service:").is_some_and(|name| !name.is_empty()) => Some(principal),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    /// Remove only entire service disjuncts; a conjunct, negation or ordinary
    /// principal equality must still pass the ordinary exact-audience check.
    pub fn device_audience_rule(&self) -> std::borrow::Cow<'_, RuleExpr> {
        let terms = self.disjuncts();
        if !terms.iter().any(|term| term.service_grant().is_some()) { return std::borrow::Cow::Borrowed(self); }

}

impl RuleExpr {
    pub fn has_reference(&self) -> bool {
        self.any_term(&|t| t.reference_source().is_some())
    }
    pub fn any_term(&self, term: &impl Fn(&RuleTerm) -> bool) -> bool {
        match self {
            Self::Eq(a,b) | Self::Ne(a,b) | Self::Compare(_,a,b) => term(a) || term(b),
            Self::Exists { key, equalities, .. } | Self::Created { key, equalities, .. } => key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).any(term),
            Self::And(rs) | Self::Or(rs) => rs.iter().any(|r| r.any_term(term)),
            Self::Not(r) => r.any_term(term),
            _ => false,
        }
    }
}

    let Some((column, old)) = term.reference_source() else { return; };
    let depth = term.references().count();
    let mut valid = !path.ends_with(".read") && (!old || update) && (1..=MAX_RULE_HOPS).contains(&depth)
